1→/**
     2→ * useSegmentOutputStrip - Orchestrator hook for SegmentOutputStrip.
     3→ *
     4→ * Composes sub-hooks for deletion, scrubbing, and lightbox concerns.
     5→ * Pure functions handle display slot computation and source info extraction.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import { useCallback, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
     9→import { useSourceImageChanges } from '@/shared/hooks/useSourceImageChanges';
    10→import { useMarkVariantViewed } from '@/shared/hooks/useMarkVariantViewed';
    11→import { TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET } from '../constants';
    12→import type { SegmentSlot } from '@/shared/hooks/segments';
    13→import type { PairData } from '@/shared/types/pairData';
    14→
    15→import { useSegmentDeletion } from './useSegmentDeletion';
    16→import { useSegmentScrubbing } from './useSegmentScrubbing';
    17→import { useSegmentLightbox } from './useSegmentLightbox';
    18→
    19→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→// Types
    21→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22→
    23→interface PairInfo {
    24→  index: number;
    25→  startId: string;
    26→  endId: string;
    27→  startFrame: number;
    28→  endFrame: number;
    29→  frames: number;
    30→}
    31→
    32→interface TrailingSegmentMode {
    33→  imageId: string;
    34→  imageFrame: number;
    35→  endFrame: number;
    36→}
    37→
    38→/** A display slot merged with its pixel position — ready for rendering */
    39→interface PositionedSlot {
    40→  slot: SegmentSlot;
    41→  leftPercent: number;
    42→  widthPercent: number;
    43→  isTrailing: boolean;
    44→}
    45→
    46→interface UseSegmentOutputStripProps {
    47→  shotId: string;
    48→  projectAspectRatio?: string;
    49→  containerWidth: number;
    50→  fullMin: number;
    51→  fullRange: number;
    52→  pairInfo: PairInfo[];
    53→  rawSegmentSlots: SegmentSlot[];
    54→  trailingSegmentMode?: TrailingSegmentMode;
    55→  pairDataByIndex?: Map<number, PairData>;
    56→  hasPendingTaskProp?: (pairShotGenerationId: string | null | undefined) => boolean;
    57→  readOnly: boolean;
    58→  onTrailingVideoInfo?: (videoUrl: string | null) => void;
    59→  selectedParentId?: string | null;
    60→  onSegmentFrameCountChange?: (pairShotGenerationId: string, frameCount: number) => void;
    61→  isMultiImage?: boolean;
    62→  lastImageFrame?: number;
    63→}
    64→
    65→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66→// Pure functions
    67→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68→
    69→/**
    70→ * Build the ordered list of display slots from raw segment slots and pair info.
    71→ *
    72→ * Matches raw slots to pairs by pairShotGenerationId (the start-image ID),
    73→ * which stays correct during drag even when sequential indices shift.
    74→ * Falls back to index-based matching for slots without a pairShotGenerationId.
    75→ */
    76→function buildDisplaySlots(
    77→  pairInfo: PairInfo[],
    78→  rawSegmentSlots: SegmentSlot[],
    79→  trailingSegmentMode?: TrailingSegmentMode,
    80→): SegmentSlot[] {
    81→  // Index raw slots by pairShotGenerationId (start image ID) for drag-stable matching.
    82→  // Prefer child over placeholder when both exist for the same key.
    83→  const slotByPsgId = new Map<string, SegmentSlot>();
    84→  const slotByIndex = new Map<number, SegmentSlot>();
    85→  for (const slot of rawSegmentSlots) {
    86→    const psgId = slot.pairShotGenerationId;
    87→    if (psgId) {
    88→      const existing = slotByPsgId.get(psgId);
    89→      if (!existing || (slot.type === 'child' && existing.type !== 'child')) {
    90→        slotByPsgId.set(psgId, slot);
    91→      }
    92→    }
    93→    // Also keep index-based fallback
    94→    const existingByIndex = slotByIndex.get(slot.index);
    95→    if (!existingByIndex || (slot.type === 'child' && existingByIndex.type !== 'child')) {
    96→      slotByIndex.set(slot.index, slot);
    97→    }
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  // Walk pairs: match by startId first (drag-stable), fall back to index
   101→  const slots: SegmentSlot[] = [];
   102→
   103→  for (const pair of pairInfo) {
   104→    const raw = slotByPsgId.get(pair.startId) || slotByIndex.get(pair.index);
   105→    slots.push(raw ?? { type: 'placeholder', index: pair.index });
   106→  }
   107→
   108→  // Trailing segment (slot after all pairs)
   109→  if (trailingSegmentMode) {
   110→    const trailingIndex = pairInfo.length;
   111→    const raw = slotByPsgId.get(trailingSegmentMode.imageId) || slotByIndex.get(trailingIndex);
   112→    slots.push(raw ?? {
   113→      type: 'placeholder',
   114→      index: trailingIndex,
   115→      pairShotGenerationId: trailingSegmentMode.imageId,
   116→      expectedFrames: trailingSegmentMode.endFrame - trailingSegmentMode.imageFrame,
   117→    });
   118→  }
   119→
   120→  return slots;
   121→}
   122→
   123→/**
   124→ * Extract source image info from child segment slots for mismatch detection.
   125→ * Parses task params to find which generation IDs were used as inputs.
   126→ */
   127→function buildSegmentSourceInfo(rawSegmentSlots: SegmentSlot[]) {
   128→  return rawSegmentSlots
   129→    .filter((slot): slot is SegmentSlot & { type: 'child' } => slot.type === 'child')
   130→    .map(slot => {
   131→      const child = slot.child;
   132→      const params = child.params as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   133→      const individualParams = (params?.individual_segment_params || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   134→      const orchDetails = (params?.orchestrator_details || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   135→      const childOrder = child.child_order ?? slot.index;
   136→      const orchGenIds = (orchDetails.input_image_generation_ids || []) as string[];
   137→      const startGenId = (individualParams.start_image_generation_id as string)
   138→        || (params?.start_image_generation_id as string)
   139→        || orchGenIds[childOrder]
   140→        || null;
   141→      const endGenId = (individualParams.end_image_generation_id as string)
   142→        || (params?.end_image_generation_id as string)
   143→        || orchGenIds[childOrder + 1]
   144→        || null;
   145→      return { segmentId: child.id, childOrder, params: params || {}, startGenId, endGenId };
   146→    });
   147→}
   148→
   149→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→// Hook
   151→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   152→
   153→export function useSegmentOutputStrip({
   154→  shotId,
   155→  projectAspectRatio,
   156→  containerWidth,
   157→  fullMin,
   158→  fullRange,
   159→  pairInfo,
   160→  rawSegmentSlots,
   161→  trailingSegmentMode,
   162→  pairDataByIndex,
   163→  hasPendingTaskProp,
   164→  readOnly,
   165→  onTrailingVideoInfo,
   166→  selectedParentId,
   167→  onSegmentFrameCountChange,
   168→  isMultiImage,
   169→  lastImageFrame,
   170→}: UseSegmentOutputStripProps) {
   171→  // ===== DISPLAY SLOTS =====
   172→  const displaySlots = useMemo(
   173→    () => buildDisplaySlots(pairInfo, rawSegmentSlots, trailingSegmentMode),
   174→    [pairInfo, rawSegmentSlots, trailingSegmentMode],
   175→  );
   176→
   177→  // ===== SUB-HOOKS =====
   178→  const deletion = useSegmentDeletion();
   179→  const scrubbingHook = useSegmentScrubbing({ projectAspectRatio, displaySlots });
   180→  const lightbox = useSegmentLightbox({ displaySlots, pairDataByIndex, selectedParentId });
   181→  const { clearScrubbing } = scrubbingHook;
   182→  const { setLightboxIndex } = lightbox;
   183→  const { markAllViewed } = useMarkVariantViewed();
   184→
   185→  // ===== PENDING TASK CHECKER =====
   186→  const hasPendingTask = hasPendingTaskProp ?? (() => false);
   187→
   188→  // ===== SOURCE IMAGE CHANGE DETECTION =====
   189→  const segmentSourceInfo = useMemo(() => buildSegmentSourceInfo(rawSegmentSlots), [rawSegmentSlots]);
   190→  const { hasRecentMismatch } = useSourceImageChanges(segmentSourceInfo, !readOnly);
   191→
   192→  // ===== SEGMENT CLICK =====
   193→  const handleSegmentClick = useCallback((
   194→    slot: SegmentSlot,
   195→    slotIndex: number,
   196→    onOpenPairSettings?: (pairIndex: number, pairFrameData?: { frames: number; startFrame: number; endFrame: number }) => void,
   197→  ) => {
   198→    clearScrubbing();
   199→    if (slot.type === 'child') {
   200→      markAllViewed(slot.child.id);
   201→    }
   202→
   203→    if (onOpenPairSettings) {
   204→      // Route all clicks through pair settings → segmentSlotMode lightbox.
   205→      // This handles both regular pairs and trailing segments uniformly,
   206→      // with proper form-only and video views plus full chevron navigation.
   207→      const pairFrameData = pairInfo.find(p => p.index === slot.index);
   208→      onOpenPairSettings(slot.index, pairFrameData ? {
   209→        frames: pairFrameData.frames,
   210→        startFrame: pairFrameData.startFrame,
   211→        endFrame: pairFrameData.endFrame,
   212→      } : undefined);
   213→    } else if (slot.type === 'child') {
   214→      // No pair settings handler — fall back to inline SegmentOutputStrip lightbox
   215→      setLightboxIndex(slotIndex);
   216→    }
   217→  }, [markAllViewed, pairInfo, clearScrubbing, setLightboxIndex]);
   218→
   219→  // ===== TRAILING VIDEO INFO REPORTING =====
   220→  // Trailing slot is always the last displaySlot when trailingSegmentMode is active
   221→  useEffect(() => {
   222→    if (!onTrailingVideoInfo || !trailingSegmentMode) {
   223→      onTrailingVideoInfo?.(null);
   224→      return;
   225→    }
   226→    const trailingSlot = displaySlots.length > pairInfo.length
   227→      ? displaySlots[displaySlots.length - 1]
   228→      : undefined;
   229→    if (trailingSlot?.type === 'child' && trailingSlot.child.location) {
   230→      onTrailingVideoInfo(trailingSlot.child.location);
   231→    } else {
   232→      onTrailingVideoInfo(null);
   233→    }
   234→  }, [displaySlots, pairInfo.length, trailingSegmentMode, onTrailingVideoInfo]);
   235→
   236→  // ===== POSITIONED SLOTS (slot + pixel position in single computation) =====
   237→  // Uses array index (not slot.index) to pair displaySlots with pairInfo,
   238→  // since buildDisplaySlots outputs one slot per pair in matching order.
   239→  const positionedSlots = useMemo((): PositionedSlot[] => {
   240→    if (fullRange <= 0 || containerWidth <= 0) return [];
   241→    const effectiveWidth = containerWidth - (TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2);
   242→
   243→    const result = displaySlots.map((slot, i) => {
   244→      const isTrailing = i >= pairInfo.length && !!trailingSegmentMode;
   245→
   246→      let startFrame: number;
   247→      let endFrame: number;
   248→      if (isTrailing) {
   249→        startFrame = trailingSegmentMode!.imageFrame;
   250→        endFrame = trailingSegmentMode!.endFrame;
   251→      } else {
   252→        const pair = pairInfo[i];
   253→        if (!pair) return { slot, leftPercent: 0, widthPercent: 0, isTrailing: false };
   254→        startFrame = pair.startFrame;
   255→        endFrame = pair.endFrame;
   256→      }
   257→
   258→      const startPixel = TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET + ((startFrame - fullMin) / fullRange) * effectiveWidth;
   259→      const endPixel = TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET + ((endFrame - fullMin) / fullRange) * effectiveWidth;
   260→      const width = endPixel - startPixel;
   261→
   262→      return {
   263→        slot,
   264→        leftPercent: (startPixel / containerWidth) * 100,
   265→        widthPercent: (width / containerWidth) * 100,
   266→        isTrailing,
   267→      };
   268→    });
   269→
   270→    return result;
   271→  }, [displaySlots, pairInfo, fullMin, fullRange, containerWidth, trailingSegmentMode]);
   272→
   273→  // ===== ADD TRAILING BUTTON POSITION =====
   274→  const addTrailingButtonLeftPercent = useMemo((): number | null => {
   275→    if (!isMultiImage || lastImageFrame === undefined || fullRange <= 0 || containerWidth <= 0) return null;
   276→    // Trailing slot exists when displaySlots has more items than pairInfo
   277→    if (displaySlots.length > pairInfo.length) return null;
   278→    const effectiveWidth = containerWidth - (TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2);
   279→    const lastImagePixel = TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET + ((lastImageFrame - fullMin) / fullRange) * effectiveWidth;
   280→    return (lastImagePixel / containerWidth) * 100;
   281→  }, [isMultiImage, lastImageFrame, pairInfo.length, displaySlots.length, fullRange, containerWidth, fullMin]);
   282→
   283→  return {
   284→    // Refs
   285→    previewVideoRef: scrubbingHook.previewVideoRef,
   286→    stripContainerRef: scrubbingHook.stripContainerRef,
   287→
   288→    // Mobile
   289→    isMobile: scrubbingHook.isMobile,
   290→
   291→    // Display data
   292→    positionedSlots,
   293→    addTrailingButtonLeftPercent,
   294→    hasPendingTask,
   295→    hasRecentMismatch,
   296→
   297→    // Scrubbing
   298→    activeScrubbingIndex: scrubbingHook.activeScrubbingIndex,
   299→    activeSegmentSlot: scrubbingHook.activeSegmentSlot,
   300→    activeSegmentVideoUrl: scrubbingHook.activeSegmentVideoUrl,
   301→    scrubbing: scrubbingHook.scrubbing,
   302→    previewPosition: scrubbingHook.previewPosition,
   303→    previewDimensions: scrubbingHook.previewDimensions,
   304→    clampedPreviewX: scrubbingHook.clampedPreviewX,
   305→    handleScrubbingStart: scrubbingHook.handleScrubbingStart,
   306→
   307→    // Lightbox
   308→    lightboxIndex: lightbox.lightboxIndex,
   309→    lightboxMedia: lightbox.lightboxMedia,
   310→    lightboxCurrentSegmentImages: lightbox.lightboxCurrentSegmentImages,
   311→    lightboxCurrentFrameCount: lightbox.lightboxCurrentFrameCount,
   312→    currentLightboxMedia: lightbox.currentLightboxMedia,
   313→    childSlotIndices: lightbox.childSlotIndices,
   314→    handleSegmentClick,
   315→    handleLightboxNext: lightbox.handleLightboxNext,
   316→    handleLightboxPrev: lightbox.handleLightboxPrev,
   317→    handleLightboxClose: lightbox.handleLightboxClose,
   318→
   319→    // Deletion
   320→    deletingSegmentId: deletion.deletingSegmentId,
   321→    handleDeleteSegment: deletion.handleDeleteSegment,
   322→
   323→    // Segment frame count change (pass-through)
   324→    onSegmentFrameCountChange,
   325→    shotId,
   326→  };
   327→}
   328→

1→/**
     2→ * InlineSegmentVideo - Individual segment thumbnail in the output strip.
     3→ *
     4→ * The component routes render behavior through focused view modules:
     5→ * - SegmentPlaceholder: no child generation yet
     6→ * - SegmentProcessing: child exists but no output
     7→ * - SegmentPreview: full thumbnail/hover preview UI
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import type React from 'react';
    11→import { SegmentPlaceholder } from './inline-segment-video/SegmentPlaceholder';
    12→import { SegmentPreview } from './inline-segment-video/SegmentPreview';
    13→import { SegmentProcessing } from './inline-segment-video/SegmentProcessing';
    14→import type { InlineSegmentVideoProps } from './inline-segment-video/types';
    15→import { useLayoutProps } from './inline-segment-video/useLayoutProps';
    16→import { useSegmentBadge } from './inline-segment-video/useSegmentBadge';
    17→
    18→export const InlineSegmentVideo: React.FC<InlineSegmentVideoProps> = ({
    19→  slot,
    20→  pairIndex,
    21→  onClick,
    22→  projectAspectRatio,
    23→  isMobile = false,
    24→  leftPercent,
    25→  widthPercent,
    26→  layout = 'absolute',
    27→  compact = false,
    28→  onOpenPairSettings,
    29→  onDelete,
    30→  isDeleting = false,
    31→  isPending = false,
    32→  hasSourceChanged = false,
    33→  isScrubbingActive = false,
    34→  onScrubbingStart,
    35→  scrubbingContainerRef,
    36→  scrubbingContainerProps,
    37→  scrubbingProgress,
    38→  readOnly = false,
    39→}) => {
    40→  const layoutProps = useLayoutProps(layout, compact, isMobile, leftPercent, widthPercent);
    41→
    42→  const isPlaceholder = slot.type === 'placeholder';
    43→  const child = slot.type === 'child' ? slot.child : null;
    44→
    45→  const {
    46→    badgeData,
    47→    showNewBadge,
    48→    isNewWithNoVariants,
    49→    unviewedCount,
    50→    onMarkAllViewed,
    51→  } = useSegmentBadge(child);
    52→
    53→  if (isPlaceholder) {
    54→    return (
    55→      <SegmentPlaceholder
    56→        layoutProps={layoutProps}
    57→        isPending={isPending}
    58→        readOnly={readOnly}
    59→        pairIndex={pairIndex}
    60→        onOpenPairSettings={onOpenPairSettings}
    61→      />
    62→    );
    63→  }
    64→
    65→  if (!child || !child.location) {
    66→    return (
    67→      <SegmentProcessing
    68→        layoutProps={layoutProps}
    69→        isPending={isPending}
    70→        pairIndex={pairIndex}
    71→        onOpenPairSettings={onOpenPairSettings}
    72→      />
    73→    );
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  return (
    77→    <SegmentPreview
    78→      layoutProps={layoutProps}
    79→      child={child}
    80→      pairIndex={pairIndex}
    81→      onClick={onClick}
    82→      projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
    83→      onDelete={onDelete}
    84→      isDeleting={isDeleting}
    85→      isPending={isPending}
    86→      hasSourceChanged={hasSourceChanged}
    87→      scrubbing={{
    88→        isActive: isScrubbingActive,
    89→        onStart: onScrubbingStart,
    90→        containerRef: scrubbingContainerRef,
    91→        containerProps: scrubbingContainerProps,
    92→        progress: scrubbingProgress,
    93→      }}
    94→      badge={{
    95→        data: badgeData ?? null,
    96→        showNew: showNewBadge,
    97→        isNewWithNoVariants,
    98→        unviewedCount,
    99→        onMarkAllViewed,
   100→      }}
   101→    />
   102→  );
   103→};
   104→

1→import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     3→import { queryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys';
     4→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     5→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     6→import { log } from '@/shared/lib/logger';
     7→
     8→const SEGMENT_DELETE_LOG_PREFIX = '[SegmentDeletePersist]';
     9→
    10→function shortId(id: string | null | undefined): string | null {
    11→  return id ? id.slice(0, 8) : null;
    12→}
    13→
    14→export function useSegmentDeletion() {
    15→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    16→  const [deletingSegmentId, setDeletingSegmentId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    17→
    18→  const getPairShotGenIdFromParams = useCallback((params: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined) => {
    19→    if (!params) return null;
    20→    const individualParams = (params.individual_segment_params || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    21→    return (individualParams.pair_shot_generation_id as string) || (params.pair_shot_generation_id as string) || null;
    22→  }, []);
    23→
    24→  const handleDeleteSegment = useCallback(async (generationId: string) => {
    25→    setDeletingSegmentId(generationId);
    26→    try {
    27→      log(`${SEGMENT_DELETE_LOG_PREFIX} delete requested`, {
    28→        generationId: shortId(generationId),
    29→      });
    30→
    31→      const { data: beforeData } = await supabase().from('generations')
    32→        .select('id, type, parent_generation_id, location, params, primary_variant_id, pair_shot_generation_id')
    33→        .eq('id', generationId)
    34→        .single();
    35→
    36→      if (!beforeData) return;
    37→
    38→      const pairShotGenId = beforeData.pair_shot_generation_id || getPairShotGenIdFromParams(beforeData.params as Record<string, unknown> | null);
    39→      const parentId = beforeData.parent_generation_id;
    40→      const childrenQueryKey = parentId ? queryKeys.segments.children(parentId) : null;
    41→      log(`${SEGMENT_DELETE_LOG_PREFIX} delete target resolved`, {
    42→        generationId: shortId(beforeData.id),
    43→        parentGenerationId: shortId(parentId),
    44→        pairShotGenerationId: shortId(pairShotGenId),
    45→      });
    46→
    47→      let idsToDelete = [generationId];
    48→      if (pairShotGenId && parentId) {
    49→        const { data: siblings } = await supabase().from('generations')
    50→          .select('id, pair_shot_generation_id, params')
    51→          .eq('parent_generation_id', parentId);
    52→        idsToDelete = (siblings || [])
    53→          .filter(child => {
    54→            const childPairId = child.pair_shot_generation_id || getPairShotGenIdFromParams(child.params as Record<string, unknown> | null);
    55→            return childPairId === pairShotGenId;
    56→          })
    57→          .map(child => child.id);
    58→        log(`${SEGMENT_DELETE_LOG_PREFIX} matched sibling segments`, {
    59→          generationId: shortId(generationId),
    60→          parentGenerationId: shortId(parentId),
    61→          pairShotGenerationId: shortId(pairShotGenId),
    62→          deleteCount: idsToDelete.length,
    63→          deleteIds: idsToDelete.map((id) => shortId(id)),
    64→        });
    65→      }
    66→
    67→      log(`${SEGMENT_DELETE_LOG_PREFIX} delete rpc start`, {
    68→        deleteCount: idsToDelete.length,
    69→        deleteIds: idsToDelete.map((id) => shortId(id)),
    70→      });
    71→      const { error: deleteError } = await supabase().from('generations')
    72→        .delete()
    73→        .in('id', idsToDelete);
    74→      if (deleteError) throw new Error(`Failed to delete: ${deleteError.message}`);
    75→      log(`${SEGMENT_DELETE_LOG_PREFIX} delete rpc succeeded`, {
    76→        deleteCount: idsToDelete.length,
    77→      });
    78→
    79→      if (childrenQueryKey) {
    80→        queryClient.setQueryData(childrenQueryKey, (oldData: unknown) => {
    81→          if (!Array.isArray(oldData)) return oldData;
    82→          return (oldData as Array<{ id: string }>).filter((item) => !idsToDelete.includes(item.id));
    83→        });
    84→      }
    85→
    86→      // Optimistic cache update
    87→      queryClient.setQueriesData(
    88→        { predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === queryKeys.segments.childrenAll[0] },
    89→        (oldData: unknown) => {
    90→          if (!oldData || !Array.isArray(oldData)) return oldData;
    91→          return (oldData as Array<{ id: string }>).filter((item) => !idsToDelete.includes(item.id));
    92→        }
    93→      );
    94→
    95→      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({
    96→        predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === queryKeys.segments.childrenAll[0],
    97→        refetchType: 'all'
    98→      });
    99→      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   100→        predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === queryKeys.segments.parentsAll[0],
   101→        refetchType: 'all'
   102→      });
   103→      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   104→      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   105→      log(`${SEGMENT_DELETE_LOG_PREFIX} cache invalidation complete`, {
   106→        generationId: shortId(generationId),
   107→      });
   108→    } catch (error) {
   109→      log(`${SEGMENT_DELETE_LOG_PREFIX} delete failed`, {
   110→        generationId: shortId(generationId),
   111→        error,
   112→      });
   113→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'SegmentDelete', toastTitle: 'Failed to delete segment' });
   114→    } finally {
   115→      setDeletingSegmentId(null);
   116→    }
   117→  }, [getPairShotGenIdFromParams, queryClient]);
   118→
   119→  return { deletingSegmentId, handleDeleteSegment };
   120→}
   121→

   141→    if (video.duration) {
   142→      setDuration(video.duration);
   143→    }
   144→
   145→    video.addEventListener('timeupdate', handleTimeUpdate);
   146→    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', handleLoadedMetadata);
   147→
   148→    return () => {
   149→      video.removeEventListener('timeupdate', handleTimeUpdate);
   150→      video.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', handleLoadedMetadata);
   151→    };
   152→  }, [isHovering, child.location]);
   153→
   154→  const currentFrame = Math.floor(currentTime * FRAMES_PER_SECOND);
   155→  const progressPercent = duration > 0 ? (currentTime / duration) * 100 : 0;
   156→
   157→  const containerRefToUse = isScrubbingActive && scrubbingContainerRef ? scrubbingContainerRef : localContainerRef;
   158→
   159→  return (
   160→    <>
   161→      <div
   162→        ref={containerRefToUse}
   163→        className={cn(
   164→          'cursor-pointer overflow-hidden bg-muted/20',
   165→          roundedClass,
   166→          'transition-all duration-150 relative',
   167→          containerBorderClasses,
   168→          isHovering && 'z-10 border-primary shadow-xl',
   169→          layout === 'flow' && isHovering && 'ring-primary/40',
   170→          isScrubbingActive && 'ring-2 ring-primary ring-offset-1',
   171→          flowContainerClasses,
   172→        )}
   173→        style={adjustedPositionStyle}
   174→        onClick={() => {
   175→          try {
   176→            onClick();
   177→          } catch (err) {
   178→            normalizeAndPresentError(err, { context: 'SegmentPreview.onClick' });
   179→          }
   180→        }}
   181→        onMouseEnter={handleMouseEnter}
   182→        onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}
   183→        onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
   184→      >
   185→        {thumbUrl && !imageError ? (
   186→          <img
   187→            src={getDisplayUrl(thumbUrl)}
   188→            alt={`Segment ${pairIndex + 1}`}
   189→            className={cn('absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover', isHovering && 'brightness-110')}
   190→            onError={() => setImageError(true)}
   191→          />
   192→        ) : imageError ? (
   193→          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-muted/50">
   194→            <ImageOff className={cn(layout === 'flow' && compact ? 'w-4 h-4' : 'w-8 h-8', 'text-muted-foreground')} />
   195→          </div>
   196→        ) : (
   197→          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-muted animate-pulse" />
   198→        )}
   199→
   200→        {(badgeData || showNewBadge) && (
   201→          <VariantBadge
   202→            derivedCount={badgeData?.derivedCount || 0}
   203→            unviewedVariantCount={unviewedCount}
   204→            hasUnviewedVariants={showNewBadge}
   205→            alwaysShowNew={isNewWithNoVariants}
   206→            variant="overlay"
   207→            size="lg"
   208→            position={layout === 'flow' && compact ? 'top-1.5 left-1.5' : 'top-2 left-2'}
   209→            onMarkAllViewed={handleMarkAllVariantsViewed}
   210→          />
   211→        )}
   212→
   213→        {onDelete && (
   214→          <button
   215→            className={cn(
   216→              'absolute top-1 right-1 z-20 w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center',
   217→              'bg-destructive/90 hover:bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground',
   218→              'transition-opacity duration-150',
   219→              isDeleting ? 'opacity-100' : isHovering ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0',
   220→            )}
   221→            onClick={(e) => {
   222→              e.stopPropagation();
   223→              if (!isDeleting) {
   224→                onDelete(child.id);
   225→              }
   226→            }}
   227→            disabled={isDeleting}
   228→            title="Delete segment"
   229→          >
   230→            {isDeleting ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
   231→          </button>
   232→        )}
   233→
   234→        {!isPending && (
   235→          <div className={cn('absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center', 'transition-opacity duration-150', isHovering ? 'opacity-0' : 'opacity-100')}>
   236→            <div className={cn('rounded-full bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center', layout === 'flow' && compact ? 'w-6 h-6' : 'w-12 h-12')}>
   237→              <Play className={cn('text-white ml-0.5', layout === 'flow' && compact ? 'w-3 h-3' : 'w-6 h-6')} fill="white" />
   238→            </div>
   239→          </div>
   240→        )}
   241→
   242→        {isPending && (
   243→          <div
   244→            className="absolute bottom-1 right-1 z-20 flex items-center justify-center bg-background/95 p-1.5 rounded-md border shadow-sm cursor-default"
   245→            title="A generation is pending"
   246→          >
   247→            <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-primary" />
   248→          </div>
   249→        )}
   250→
   251→        {hasSourceChanged && !isPending && (
   252→          <Tooltip>
   253→            <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   254→              <div className="absolute bottom-1 left-1 z-20 flex items-center justify-center bg-amber-500/90 hover:bg-amber-600/95 p-1 rounded-md shadow-sm cursor-default transition-colors">
   255→                <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3 text-white" />
   256→              </div>
   257→            </TooltipTrigger>
   258→            <TooltipContent side="top" className="max-w-xs">
   259→              <p>Source image has changed since this video was created. Consider regenerating.</p>
   260→            </TooltipContent>
   261→          </Tooltip>
   262→        )}
   263→
   264→        {isHovering && <div className="absolute inset-0 ring-2 ring-primary ring-inset pointer-events-none" />}
   265→
   266→        {isHovering && (scrubbingProgress !== undefined || duration > 0) && (
   267→          <div className={cn('absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-black/30 pointer-events-none', layout === 'flow' && compact ? 'h-0.5' : 'h-1')}>
   268→            <div
   269→              className="h-full bg-primary transition-all duration-75"
   270→              style={{ width: `${scrubbingProgress !== undefined ? scrubbingProgress * 100 : progressPercent}%` }}
   271→            />
   272→          </div>
   273→        )}
   274→      </div>
   275→
   276→      {isHovering &&
   277→        !isMobile &&
   278→        videoUrl &&
   279→        !isScrubbingActive &&
   280→        createPortal(
   281→          <div
   282→            className="fixed pointer-events-none"
   283→            style={{
   284→              left: `${hoverPosition.x}px`,
   285→              top: `${hoverPosition.y - estimatedPreviewHeight - 40}px`,
   286→              transform: 'translateX(-50%)',
   287→              zIndex: 999999,
   288→            }}
   289→          >
   290→            <div className="bg-background border-2 border-primary rounded-lg shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
   291→              <div className="relative bg-black" style={{ width: previewStyle.width, aspectRatio: previewStyle.aspectRatio }}>
   292→                <video
   293→                  ref={videoRef}
   294→                  src={getDisplayUrl(videoUrl)}
   295→                  className="w-full h-full object-cover"
   296→                  muted
   297→                  loop
   298→                  playsInline
   299→                  preload="auto"
   300→                />
   301→              </div>
   302→
   303→              <div className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-background/95 border-t border-primary/40 flex items-center justify-between">
   304→                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground">Segment {pairIndex + 1}</span>
   305→                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   306→                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">Frame {currentFrame}</span>
   307→                  {(badgeData || showNewBadge) && (
   308→                    <VariantBadge
   309→                      derivedCount={badgeData?.derivedCount || 0}
   310→                      unviewedVariantCount={unviewedCount}
   311→                      hasUnviewedVariants={showNewBadge}
   312→                      alwaysShowNew={isNewWithNoVariants}
   313→                      variant="inline"
   314→                      size="md"
   315→                      tooltipSide="top"
   316→                      onMarkAllViewed={handleMarkAllVariantsViewed}
   317→                    />
   318→                  )}
   319→                </div>
   320→              </div>
   321→            </div>
   322→          </div>,
   323→          document.body,
   324→        )}
   325→    </>
   326→  );
   327→};
   328→
```

The entire flow works like this:
1. User hovers over segment thumbnail → delete button becomes visible
2. User clicks delete button → `onDelete(child.id)` called
3. `handleDeleteSegment()` executes: fetches sibling segments with same pair ID, deletes all, updates cache
4. Loading spinner displays during deletion
5. On error, toast notification shown via error handler

1→/**
     2→ * LightboxShell - Dialog/Overlay container for MediaLightbox
     3→ *
     4→ * Encapsulates all the complex event handling for the lightbox modal:
     5→ * - Dialog root/portal/overlay
     6→ * - Pointer/touch/click event handling with z-index awareness
     7→ * - Body scroll locking
     8→ * - Accessibility elements
     9→ *
    10→ * Note: Tasks pane controls are handled by the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane,
    11→ * which is visible above the lightbox at z-[100001]. The overlay adjusts its size
    12→ * to account for the pane when it's open or locked.
    13→ *
    14→ * This allows the main MediaLightbox to focus on content orchestration.
    15→ */
    16→
    17→import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
    18→import { Dialog as DialogPrimitive } from "@base-ui-components/react/dialog";
    19→import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
    20→import { useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers } from '@/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers';
    21→import { useLightboxViewportLock } from '@/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useLightboxViewportLock';
    22→import { useLightboxPaneLayout } from '@/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useLightboxPaneLayout';
    23→import type { OverlayViewportConstraints } from '@/features/layout/contracts/overlayViewportConstraints';
    24→import { UI_Z_LAYERS } from '@/shared/lib/uiLayers';
    25→
    26→interface LightboxShellProps {
    27→  children: React.ReactNode;
    28→  onClose: () => void;
    29→
    30→  // Edit mode
    31→  /** Canvas/brush edit overlay is active (inpaint, annotate, reposition, text).
    32→   *  Used to allow canvas touch events to pass through. */
    33→  hasCanvasOverlay: boolean;
    34→  /** Reposition (move) mode — the only edit mode that blocks background click-to-close
    35→   *  (because dragging could cause accidental closes). */
    36→  isRepositionMode: boolean;
    37→
    38→  // Responsive
    39→  isMobile: boolean;
    40→  isTabletOrLarger: boolean;
    41→
    42→  // Layout constraints from the layout subsystem.
    43→  overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints;
    44→
    45→  // Ref for content
    46→  contentRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
    47→
    48→  // Accessibility
    49→  accessibilityTitle: string;
    50→  accessibilityDescription: string;
    51→}
    52→
    53→export const LightboxShell: React.FC<LightboxShellProps> = ({
    54→  children,
    55→  onClose,
    56→  hasCanvasOverlay,
    57→  isRepositionMode,
    58→  isMobile,
    59→  isTabletOrLarger,
    60→  overlayViewport,
    61→  contentRef,
    62→  accessibilityTitle,
    63→  accessibilityDescription,
    64→}) => {
    65→  const isActuallyModal = isMobile && !isTabletOrLarger;
    66→  useLightboxViewportLock({ isActuallyModal });
    67→
    68→  const {
    69→    handleOverlayPointerDown,
    70→    handleOverlayPointerUp,
    71→    handleOverlayClick,
    72→    handleBgPointerDownCapture,
    73→    handleBgClickCapture,
    74→    handleContentPointerDown,
    75→    handleContentClick,
    76→    handleTouchEvent,
    77→    handleTouchCancel,
    78→  } = useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers({
    79→    hasCanvasOverlay,
    80→    isRepositionMode,
    81→    isMobile,
    82→    onClose,
    83→  });
    84→
    85→  // Focus the content element on mount
    86→  useEffect(() => {
    87→    contentRef.current?.focus();
    88→  }, [contentRef]);
    89→
    90→  const { overlayStyle, contentStyle } = useLightboxPaneLayout({
    91→    overlayViewport,
    92→  });
    93→
    94→  return (
    95→    <DialogPrimitive.Root
    96→      open={true}
    97→      modal={isActuallyModal}
    98→      disablePointerDismissal
    99→      onOpenChange={(_open, eventDetails) => {
   100→        // Prevent Base UI from updating internal state (which corrupts controlled open={true})
   101→        // and from calling stopPropagation on the native event (which blocks our custom handlers).
   102→        // Our own handlers in useLightboxNavigation (Escape) and handleOverlayClick (outside press)
   103→        // manage all closing logic.
   104→        eventDetails.cancel();
   105→        eventDetails.allowPropagation();
   106→      }}
   107→    >
   108→      <DialogPrimitive.Portal>
   109→        <DialogPrimitive.Backdrop
   110→          data-dialog-backdrop
   111→          className={cn(
   112→            "fixed bg-black/80 p-0 border-none shadow-none",
   113→            "data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0",
   114→            !isMobile && "duration-200"
   115→          )}
   116→          onPointerDown={handleOverlayPointerDown}
   117→          onPointerUp={handleOverlayPointerUp}
   118→          onClick={handleOverlayClick}
   119→          onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
   120→          onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
   121→          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
   122→          onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
   123→          style={{
   124→            ...overlayStyle,
   125→            zIndex: UI_Z_LAYERS.LIGHTBOX_MODAL,
   126→          }}
   127→        />
   128→
   129→        {/* Task pane handle removed - the existing PaneControlTab from TasksPane
   130→            is now visible above the lightbox and handles all pane controls.
   131→            The overlay correctly accounts for the pane via shouldAccountForTasksPane. */}
   132→
   133→        <DialogPrimitive.Popup
   134→          ref={contentRef}
   135→          tabIndex={-1}
   136→          onPointerDownCapture={handleBgPointerDownCapture}
   137→          onClickCapture={handleBgClickCapture}
   138→          onPointerDown={handleContentPointerDown}
   139→          onClick={handleContentClick}
   140→          onTouchStart={handleTouchEvent}
   141→          onTouchMove={handleTouchEvent}
   142→          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEvent}
   143→          onTouchCancel={handleTouchCancel}
   144→          className={cn(
   145→            "fixed",
   146→            isMobile
   147→              ? ""
   148→              : "duration-200 data-[open]:animate-in data-[ending-style]:animate-out data-[ending-style]:fade-out-0 data-[open]:fade-in-0 data-[ending-style]:zoom-out-95 data-[open]:zoom-in-95",
   149→            "p-0 border-none bg-transparent shadow-none",
   150→            overlayViewport.needsFullscreenLayout
   151→              ? "inset-0 w-full h-full"
   152→              : "left-[50%] top-[50%] translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] w-auto h-auto data-[ending-style]:slide-out-to-left-1/2 data-[ending-style]:slide-out-to-top-[48%] data-[open]:slide-in-from-left-1/2 data-[open]:slide-in-from-top-[48%]"
   153→          )}
   154→          style={{
   155→            ...contentStyle,
   156→            zIndex: UI_Z_LAYERS.LIGHTBOX_MODAL,
   157→          }}
   158→        >
   159→          {/* Accessibility: Hidden dialog title for screen readers */}
   160→          <DialogPrimitive.Title className="sr-only">
   161→            {accessibilityTitle}
   162→          </DialogPrimitive.Title>
   163→          <DialogPrimitive.Description className="sr-only">
   164→            {accessibilityDescription}
   165→          </DialogPrimitive.Description>
   166→
   167→          {children}
   168→        </DialogPrimitive.Popup>
   169→      </DialogPrimitive.Portal>
   170→    </DialogPrimitive.Root>
   171→  );
   172→};
   173→
```

    35→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string, derivedContext?: string[]) => Promise<void>;
    36→  onVariantSelect?: (variantId: string) => void;
    37→  currentMediaId?: string;
    38→  currentShotId?: string;
    39→  derivedGenerations?: GenerationRow[] | null;
    40→  activeVariant?: VariantInfo | null;
    41→  primaryVariant?: VariantInfo | null;
    42→  onSwitchToPrimary?: () => void;
    43→}
    44→
    45→/**
    46→ * TaskDetailsPanelWrapper Component
    47→ * Wraps TaskDetailsPanel with all the standard props wiring
    48→ * Includes the derived generations section
    49→ */
    50→export const TaskDetailsPanelWrapper: React.FC<TaskDetailsPanelWrapperProps> = ({
    51→  taskDetailsData,
    52→  replaceImages,
    53→  onReplaceImagesChange,
    54→  onClose,
    55→}) => {
    56→  return (
    57→    <TaskDetailsPanel
    58→      task={taskDetailsData?.task ?? null}
    59→      isLoading={taskDetailsData?.isLoading || false}
    60→      error={taskDetailsData?.error ?? null}
    61→      inputImages={taskDetailsData?.inputImages || []}
    62→      taskId={taskDetailsData?.taskId || null}
    63→      replaceImages={replaceImages}
    64→      onReplaceImagesChange={onReplaceImagesChange}
    65→      onApplySettingsFromTask={taskDetailsData?.onApplySettingsFromTask ? (taskId, replaceImages, inputImages) => {
    66→        taskDetailsData.onApplySettingsFromTask?.(taskId, replaceImages, inputImages);
    67→        onClose(); // Close lightbox after applying settings
    68→      } : undefined}
    69→      className=""
    70→      showUserImage={false}
    71→      derivedSection={null}
    72→      hideHeader={true}
    73→    />
    74→  );
    75→};

1→import React from 'react';
     2→import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
     3→import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/shared/components/ui/tooltip';
     4→import { Settings } from 'lucide-react';
     5→import { ShotSelectorControls } from './ShotSelectorControls';
     6→import type { ShotOption } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     7→
     8→export interface WorkflowControlsBarProps {
     9→  // Visibility
    10→  // CRITICAL: targetShotId is the shot selected in the DROPDOWN, not the shot being viewed
    11→  onAddToShot?: (targetShotId: string, generationId: string, imageUrl?: string, thumbUrl?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    12→  onDelete?: (id: string) => void;
    13→  onApplySettings?: (metadata: Record<string, unknown>) => void;

    26→  onCreateShot?: (shotName: string, files: File[]) => Promise<{shotId?: string; shotName?: string} | void>;
    27→  
    28→  // Shot positioning
    29→  isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot: boolean;
    30→  isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition: boolean;
    31→  showTickForImageId?: string | null;
    32→  showTickForSecondaryImageId?: string | null;
    33→  
    34→  // Shot actions
    35→  // CRITICAL: targetShotId is the shot selected in the DROPDOWN, not the shot being viewed
    36→  onAddToShotWithoutPosition?: (targetShotId: string, generationId: string, imageUrl?: string, thumbUrl?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    37→  
    38→  // Optimistic updates
    39→  onShowTick?: (imageId: string) => void;
    40→  onOptimisticPositioned?: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => void;
    41→  onShowSecondaryTick?: (imageId: string) => void;
    42→  onOptimisticUnpositioned?: (imageId: string, shotId: string) => void;
    43→  
    44→  // Loading states
    45→  isAdding?: boolean;
    46→  isAddingWithoutPosition?: boolean;
    47→
    48→  // UI state
    49→  setIsSelectOpen?: (isOpen: boolean) => void;
    50→  contentRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
    51→
    52→  // Apply settings
    53→  handleApplySettings: () => void;
    54→
    55→  // Navigation
    56→  onNavigateToShot?: (shot: ShotOption) => void;
    57→
    58→  // Close lightbox
    59→  onClose?: () => void;
    60→
    61→  // Variant promotion - for adding a variant as a new generation to a shot
    62→  onAddVariantAsNewGeneration?: (shotId: string, variantId: string, currentTimelineFrame?: number) => Promise<boolean>;
    63→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    64→  currentTimelineFrame?: number;
    65→}
    66→
    67→/**
    68→ * WorkflowControlsBar Component
    69→ * The bottom bar containing shot selector controls and apply settings button
    70→ * Used across all layout variants (Desktop Side Panel, Mobile Stacked, Regular)
    71→ */
    72→export const WorkflowControlsBar: React.FC<WorkflowControlsBarProps> = ({
    73→  onAddToShot,
    74→  onDelete,
    75→  onApplySettings,
    76→  isVideo,
    77→  mediaId,
    78→  imageUrl,
    79→  thumbUrl,
    80→  allShots,
    81→  selectedShotId,
    82→  onShotChange,
    83→  onCreateShot,
    84→  isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
    85→  isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
    86→  showTickForImageId,
    87→  showTickForSecondaryImageId,
    88→  onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    89→  onShowTick,
    90→  onOptimisticPositioned,
    91→  onShowSecondaryTick,
    92→  onOptimisticUnpositioned,
    93→  isAdding = false,
    94→  isAddingWithoutPosition = false,
    95→  setIsSelectOpen,
    96→  contentRef,
    97→  handleApplySettings,
    98→  onNavigateToShot,
    99→  onClose,
   100→  onAddVariantAsNewGeneration,
   101→  activeVariantId,
   102→  currentTimelineFrame,
   103→}) => {
   104→  // Track if shots loaded after initial render (race condition detection)
   105→  const prevShotsLengthRef = React.useRef(allShots?.length || 0);
   106→  const currentShotsLength = allShots?.length || 0;
   107→  
   108→  React.useEffect(() => {
   109→    prevShotsLengthRef.current = currentShotsLength;
   110→  }, [currentShotsLength, onAddToShot, isVideo]);
   111→  
   112→  // Don't render if no workflow actions available
   113→  if (!(onAddToShot || onDelete || onApplySettings)) {
   114→    return null;
   115→  }
   116→
   117→  return (
   118→    <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 flex items-center gap-x-2 z-[60]">
   119→      <div className="bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-lg px-1.5 py-1 flex items-center gap-x-2">
   120→        {/* Shot Selection and Add to Shot */}
   121→        {onAddToShot && allShots.length > 0 && !isVideo && (
   122→          <ShotSelectorControls
   123→            mediaId={mediaId}
   124→            imageUrl={imageUrl}
   125→            thumbUrl={thumbUrl}
   126→            allShots={allShots}
   127→            selectedShotId={selectedShotId}
   128→            onShotChange={onShotChange}
   129→            onCreateShot={onCreateShot}
   130→            isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot={isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot}
   131→            isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition={isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition}
   132→            showTickForImageId={showTickForImageId}
   133→            showTickForSecondaryImageId={showTickForSecondaryImageId}
   134→            onAddToShot={onAddToShot}
   135→            onAddToShotWithoutPosition={onAddToShotWithoutPosition}
   136→            onAddVariantAsNewGeneration={onAddVariantAsNewGeneration}
   137→            activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
   138→            currentTimelineFrame={currentTimelineFrame}
   139→            onShowTick={onShowTick}
   140→            onOptimisticPositioned={onOptimisticPositioned}
   141→            onShowSecondaryTick={onShowSecondaryTick}
   142→            onOptimisticUnpositioned={onOptimisticUnpositioned}
   143→            isAdding={isAdding}
   144→            isAddingWithoutPosition={isAddingWithoutPosition}
   145→            setIsSelectOpen={setIsSelectOpen}
   146→            contentRef={contentRef}
   147→            onNavigateToShot={onNavigateToShot}
   148→            onClose={onClose}
   149→          />
   150→        )}
   151→
   152→        {/* Apply Settings */}
   153→        {onApplySettings && (
   154→          <Tooltip>
   155→            <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   156→              <Button
   157→                variant="secondary"
   158→                size="sm"
   159→                onClick={handleApplySettings}
   160→                className="bg-purple-600/80 hover:bg-purple-600 text-white h-8 px-3"
   161→              >
   162→                <Settings className="h-4 w-4" />
   163→              </Button>
   164→            </TooltipTrigger>
   165→            <TooltipContent className="z-[100001]">Apply settings</TooltipContent>
   166→          </Tooltip>
   167→        )}
   168→      </div>
   169→    </div>
   170→  );
   171→};
   172→

    74→  isVideo,

    76→  allShots,
    77→  selectedShotId,
    78→  onShotChange,
    79→  onCreateShot,
    80→  contentRef,
    81→  isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
    82→  isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
    83→  showTickForImageId,
    84→  showTickForSecondaryImageId,
    85→  onAddToShot,
    86→  onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
    87→  onShowTick,
    88→  onOptimisticPositioned,
    89→  onShowSecondaryTick,
    90→  onOptimisticUnpositioned,
    91→  isAdding = false,
    92→  isAddingWithoutPosition = false,
    93→  onApplySettings,
    94→  handleApplySettings,
    95→  onDelete,
    96→  onClose,
    97→}) => {
    98→  const { isAddedWithoutPosition, handleAddWithoutPosition } = useShotAssociationControls({
    99→    mediaId,
   100→    imageUrl,
   101→    thumbUrl,
   102→    allShots,
   103→    selectedShotId,
   104→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
   105→    showTickForSecondaryImageId,
   106→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   107→    onShowSecondaryTick,
   108→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   109→    onNavigateToShot,
   110→    errorContext: 'WorkflowControls',
   111→  });
   112→
   113→  // Don't render if no workflow actions available, in inpaint mode, or video
   114→  if ((!onAddToShot && !onDelete && !onApplySettings) || isVideo || isInpaintMode) {
   115→    return null;
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  return (
   119→    <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 flex items-center gap-x-2 z-[60]">
   120→      <div className="bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-lg p-2 flex items-center gap-x-2">
   121→        {/* Shot Selection and Add to Shot */}
   122→        {onAddToShot && allShots.length > 0 && !isVideo && (
   123→          <>
   124→            <ShotSelectorWithAdd
   125→              imageId={mediaId}
   126→              imageUrl={imageUrl}
   127→              thumbUrl={thumbUrl}
   128→              shots={allShots}
   129→              selectedShotId={selectedShotId || ''}
   130→              onShotChange={onShotChange || (() => {})}
   131→              onAddToShot={onAddToShot}
   132→              showCreateShot={!!onCreateShot}
   133→              isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot={isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot}
   134→              showTick={showTickForImageId === mediaId}
   135→              isAdding={isAdding}
   136→              onShowTick={onShowTick}
   137→              onOptimisticPositioned={onOptimisticPositioned}
   138→              onClose={onClose}
   139→              layout="horizontal"
   140→              container={contentRef.current}
   141→              selectorClassName="w-32 h-8 bg-black/50 border-white/20 text-white text-xs"
   142→              buttonClassName="h-8 w-8"
   143→            />
   144→
   145→            {onAddToShotWithoutPosition && !isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot && (
   146→              <Tooltip>
   147→                <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   148→                  <Button
   149→                    variant="secondary"
   150→                    size="sm"
   151→                    onClick={handleAddWithoutPosition}
   152→                    disabled={!selectedShotId || isAddingWithoutPosition}
   153→                    className={`h-8 px-3 text-white ${
   154→                      isAddedWithoutPosition
   155→                        ? 'bg-green-600/80 hover:bg-green-600'
   156→                        : 'bg-purple-600/80 hover:bg-purple-600'
   157→                    }`}
   158→                  >
   159→                    {isAddingWithoutPosition ? (
   160→                      <div className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin rounded-full border-b-2 border-white"></div>
   161→                    ) : isAddedWithoutPosition ? (
   162→                      <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
   163→                    ) : (
   164→                      <PlusCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
   165→                    )}
   166→                  </Button>
   167→                </TooltipTrigger>
   168→                <TooltipContent className="z-[100001]">
   169→                  {isAddedWithoutPosition
   170→                    ? 'Added without position. Jump to shot.'
   171→                    : 'Add to shot without position'}
   172→                </TooltipContent>
   173→              </Tooltip>
   174→            )}
   175→          </>
   176→        )}
   177→
   178→        {/* Apply Settings */}
   179→        {onApplySettings && (
   180→          <Tooltip>
   181→            <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   182→              <Button
   183→                variant="secondary"
   184→                size="sm"
   185→                onClick={handleApplySettings}
   186→                className="bg-purple-600/80 hover:bg-purple-600 text-white h-8 px-3"
   187→              >
   188→                <Settings className="h-4 w-4" />
   189→              </Button>
   190→            </TooltipTrigger>
   191→            <TooltipContent className="z-[100001]">Apply settings</TooltipContent>
   192→          </Tooltip>
   193→        )}
   194→
   195→      </div>
   196→    </div>
   197→  );
   198→};
   199→
```

   100→/**

   104→ * Uses shared EditPanelLayout for consistent header and variants handling.
   105→ *
   106→ * Supports props-first pattern: optional state props (coreState, imageEditState,
   107→ * variantsState) override context values when provided. This allows
   108→ * the component to work both within ImageLightbox (using context) and standalone
   109→ * (using props, e.g., in InlineEditView).
   110→ *
   111→ * Logic (state resolution, side effects, mode config) lives in useEditModePanelState.

   113→// Default prompt for reposition mode - shown when user hasn't entered a custom prompt
   114→const REPOSITION_DEFAULT_PROMPT = 'match existing content';

   235→

   285→                        : editMode === 'reposition'

   298→                  onVoiceResult={(result) => {

   306→
   307→          {/* Img2Img Mode Controls */}
   308→          {editMode === 'img2img' && (

   312→              setImg2imgPrompt={setImg2imgPrompt}

   324→          {/* Model Selector + LoRA Selector - Shown for non-img2img/upscale edit modes */}
   325→          {editMode !== 'img2img' && editMode !== 'upscale' && editLoraManager && (
   326→            <div className={generationsSpacing}>

   328→              <div className={cn("flex items-center gap-2", isMobile ? "mb-1" : "mb-2")}>

   330→                {setQwenEditModel && (

   394→                    <SelectItem variant="retro" value="custom">Custom</SelectItem>
   395→                  </SelectContent>
   396→                </Select>
   397→                {loraMode !== 'none' && (
   398→                  <Button
   399→                    variant="ghost"
   400→                    size="sm"
   401→                    onClick={handleClearLora}
   402→                    className={cn(
   403→                      "h-9 w-9 p-0 hover:bg-muted shrink-0",

   406→                    title="Clear LoRA selection"

   408→                    <XCircle className={cn("h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground", isMobile && "h-3 w-3")} />
   409→                  </Button>
   410→                )}
   411→              </div>
   412→            </div>
   413→
   414→            {/* Custom URL Input - Show when Custom is selected */}
   415→            {loraMode === 'custom' && (
   416→              <input
   417→                type="text"
   418→                value={customLoraUrl}
   419→                onChange={(e) => setCustomLoraUrl(e.target.value)}
   420→                placeholder="https://huggingface.co/.../lora.safetensors"
   421→                className={cn(
   422→                  "w-full mt-1.5 bg-background border border-input rounded-md preserve-case",
   423→                  "focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring",

   425→                )}
   426→              />
   427→            )}
   428→          </div>
   429→          )}
   430→
   431→          {/* Advanced Settings - shown for edit modes that support hires fix (not img2img/upscale) */}
   432→          {advancedSettings && setAdvancedSettings && editMode !== 'img2img' && editMode !== 'upscale' && !isMobile && (
   433→            <EditAdvancedSettings
   434→              settings={advancedSettings}
   435→              onSettingsChange={setAdvancedSettings}
   436→              isLocalGeneration={isLocalGeneration}
   437→            />
   438→          )}
   439→
   440→          {/* Number of Generations + Create as Variant - shown for all image edit modes except upscale */}
   441→          {setCreateAsGeneration && editMode !== 'upscale' && (
   442→            <div className={cn(
   443→              "py-1.5 px-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden w-[80%]",
   444→              isMobile && "bg-muted/30"
   445→            )}>
   446→              <SectionLabel>Options</SectionLabel>
   447→              {/* Number of Generations */}
   448→              <div className={cn("flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0", isMobile ? "flex-1" : "flex-shrink")}>
   449→                <label className={cn(
   450→                  "font-medium whitespace-nowrap flex-shrink-0",
   451→                  isMobile ? "text-[10px] text-muted-foreground" : "text-sm"
   452→                )}>
   453→                  {isMobile ? '#' : 'Generations:'}
   454→                </label>
   455→                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0">
   456→                  <input
   457→                    type="range"
   458→                    min={1}
   459→                    max={16}
   460→                    value={inpaintNumGenerations}
   461→                    onChange={(e) => setInpaintNumGenerations(parseInt(e.target.value))}
   462→                    className={cn(
   463→                      "bg-muted rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-primary min-w-[60px] w-full",
   464→                      isMobile ? "h-1.5 flex-1" : "h-2 max-w-[120px]"
   465→                    )}
   466→                  />
   467→                  <span className={cn(
   468→                    "text-foreground font-medium text-center flex-shrink-0",
   469→                    isMobile ? "text-xs w-4" : "text-sm w-5"
   470→                  )}>{inpaintNumGenerations}</span>
   471→                </div>
   472→              </div>
   473→
   474→              {/* Create as Variant toggle */}
   475→              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-shrink-0">
   476→                <Tooltip>
   477→                  <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   478→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help">
   479→                      <Layers className={cn(isMobile ? "h-3 w-3" : "h-4 w-4", "text-muted-foreground")} />
   480→                      <Label htmlFor="create-as-variant" className={cn(
   481→                        "font-medium cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap",
   482→                        isMobile ? "text-[10px] text-muted-foreground" : "text-sm"
   483→                      )}>
   484→                        {isMobile ? 'Variant' : 'Variant'}
   485→                      </Label>
   486→                    </div>
   487→                  </TooltipTrigger>
   488→                  <TooltipContent side="top" className="max-w-[250px]">
   489→                    <p className="text-xs">
   490→                      <strong>On:</strong> Result appears as a variant of this image.
   491→                      <br />
   492→                      <strong>Off:</strong> Result appears as its own image in the gallery.
   493→                    </p>

   501→                />
   502→              </div>
   503→            </div>
   504→          )}
   505→
   506→          {/* Action Buttons - Skip for upscale mode (ImageUpscaleForm has its own) */}
   507→          {editMode !== 'upscale' && (editMode === 'reposition' ? (
   508→            <RepositionButtons
   509→              isMobile={isMobile}
   510→              hasTransformChanges={hasTransformChanges}
   511→              handleSaveAsVariant={handleSaveAsVariant!}
   512→              isSavingAsVariant={isSavingAsVariant}
   513→              saveAsVariantSuccess={saveAsVariantSuccess}
   514→              handleGenerateReposition={handleGenerateReposition!}

   516→              repositionGenerateSuccess={repositionGenerateSuccess}

   524→              brushStrokes={brushStrokes}

   527→              inpaintGenerateSuccess={inpaintGenerateSuccess}
   528→              isCreatingMagicEditTasks={isCreatingMagicEditTasks}
   529→              magicEditTasksCreated={magicEditTasksCreated}
   530→            />
   531→          ))}
   532→      </EditPanelLayout>
   533→
   534→      {/* Edit Mode LoRA Selector Modal (for text, inpaint, annotate, reposition modes) */}
   535→      {editLoraManager && (
   536→        <Suspense fallback={null}>
   537→          <LoraSelectorModal
   538→            isOpen={editLoraManager.isLoraModalOpen}
   539→            onClose={() => editLoraManager.setIsLoraModalOpen(false)}
   540→            loras={availableLoras}
   541→            onAddLora={editLoraManager.handleAddLora}
   542→            onRemoveLora={editLoraManager.handleRemoveLora}
   543→            onUpdateLoraStrength={editLoraManager.handleLoraStrengthChange}
   544→            selectedLoras={editLoraManager.selectedLoras.map(lora => {

   546→              return {
   547→                ...fullLora,
   548→                "Model ID": lora.id,
   549→                Name: lora.name,
   550→                strength: lora.strength,
   551→              } as LoraModel & { strength: number };
   552→            })}
   553→            lora_type="Qwen Edit"
   554→          />
   555→        </Suspense>
   556→      )}
   557→    </>
   558→  );
   559→};

   203→      {/* Scrollable content area */}

1→/**
     2→ * LightboxStateContext
     3→ *
     4→ * Provides shared state to deeply-nested lightbox components without prop drilling.
     5→ * Organized into logical domains accessible via focused hooks:
     6→ * - useLightboxCore() - onClose, readOnly, isMobile, selectedProjectId
     7→ * - useLightboxMedia() - media, isVideo, effectiveUrls, dimensions
     8→ * - useLightboxVariants() - variants, activeVariant, variant actions
     9→ * - useLightboxNavigation() - navigation state and handlers
    10→ *
    11→ * Components should prefer these hooks over prop drilling for commonly-used values.
    12→ */
    13→
    14→import React, { createContext, useContext, RefObject } from 'react';
    15→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    16→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    17→import type { CurrentSegmentImagesData } from '@/shared/components/VariantSelector/variantSourceImages';
    18→
    19→// ============================================================================
    20→// Core State
    21→// ============================================================================
    22→
    23→interface LightboxCoreState {
    24→  onClose: () => void;
    25→  readOnly: boolean;
    26→  isMobile: boolean;
    27→  isTabletOrLarger: boolean;
    28→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    29→  actualGenerationId: string | null;
    30→}
    31→
    32→// ============================================================================
    33→// Media State
    34→// ============================================================================
    35→
    36→interface LightboxMediaState {
    37→  media: GenerationRow;
    38→  isVideo: boolean;
    39→  effectiveMediaUrl: string;
    40→  effectiveVideoUrl: string;
    41→  effectiveImageDimensions: { width: number; height: number } | null;
    42→  imageDimensions: { width: number; height: number } | null;
    43→  setImageDimensions: (dims: { width: number; height: number }) => void;
    44→}
    45→
    46→// ============================================================================
    47→// Variant State
    48→// ============================================================================
    49→
    50→interface LightboxVariantState {
    51→  variants: GenerationVariant[];
    52→  activeVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
    53→  primaryVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
    54→  isLoadingVariants: boolean;
    55→  // Handler-style names for UI components
    56→  handleVariantSelect: (id: string) => void;
    57→  handleMakePrimary: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
    58→  handleDeleteVariant: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
    59→  onLoadVariantSettings?: (variantParams: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    60→  onLoadVariantImages?: (variant: GenerationVariant) => void;
    61→  currentSegmentImages?: CurrentSegmentImagesData;
    62→  // Promotion
    63→  promoteSuccess: boolean;
    64→  isPromoting: boolean;
    65→  handlePromoteToGeneration: (variantId: string) => Promise<void>;
    66→  // Make main variant
    67→  isMakingMainVariant: boolean;
    68→  canMakeMainVariant: boolean;
    69→  handleMakeMainVariant: () => Promise<void>;
    70→  // Pending/unviewed counts
    71→  pendingTaskCount: number;
    72→  unviewedVariantCount: number;
    73→  onMarkAllViewed: () => void;
    74→  variantsSectionRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement> | null;
    75→}
    76→
    77→// ============================================================================
    78→// Navigation State
    79→// ============================================================================
    80→
    81→interface LightboxNavigationState {
    82→  showNavigation: boolean;
    83→  hasNext: boolean;
    84→  hasPrevious: boolean;
    85→  handleSlotNavNext: () => void;
    86→  handleSlotNavPrev: () => void;
    87→  swipeNavigation: {
    88→    swipeHandlers: Record<string, unknown>;
    89→    isSwiping: boolean;
    90→    swipeOffset: number;
    91→  };
    92→}
    93→
    94→// ============================================================================
    95→// Combined Context Value
    96→// ============================================================================
    97→
    98→interface LightboxStateValue {
    99→  core: LightboxCoreState;
   100→  media: LightboxMediaState;
   101→  variants: LightboxVariantState;
   102→  navigation: LightboxNavigationState;
   103→}
   104→
   105→const LightboxStateContext = createContext<LightboxStateValue | null>(null);
   106→
   107→// ============================================================================
   108→// Provider Component
   109→// ============================================================================
   110→
   111→interface LightboxStateProviderProps {
   112→  children: React.ReactNode;
   113→  value: LightboxStateValue;
   114→}
   115→
   116→export const LightboxStateProvider: React.FC<LightboxStateProviderProps> = ({
   117→  children,
   118→  value,
   119→}) => {
   120→  return (
   121→    <LightboxStateContext.Provider value={value}>
   122→      {children}
   123→    </LightboxStateContext.Provider>
   124→  );
   125→};
   126→
   127→// ============================================================================
   128→// Consumer Hooks - Safe versions only (return defaults outside provider)
   129→// ============================================================================
   130→
   131→// Note: Non-safe versions (useLightboxState, useLightboxCore, etc.) are not
   132→// exported as they are not used. All internal consumers use the Safe versions
   133→// for safe operation outside the provider.
   134→
   135→// ============================================================================
   136→// Safe Hooks (for use outside provider - returns defaults)

1→/**
     2→ * useLightboxVariantBadges
     3→ *
     4→ * Computes pending task count, unviewed variant count, and mark-all-viewed handler.
     5→ * Shared between ImageLightbox and VideoLightbox (identical pattern in both).

     8→import { useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
     9→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    10→import { usePendingGenerationTasks } from '@/shared/hooks/usePendingGenerationTasks';
    11→import { useMarkVariantViewed } from '@/shared/hooks/useMarkVariantViewed';
    12→
    13→interface UseLightboxVariantBadgesInput {
    14→  /** Generation ID to check for pending tasks */
    15→  pendingTaskGenerationId: string | null;
    16→  /** Project ID for task queries */
    17→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    18→  /** Variant list (for computing unviewed count) */
    19→  variants: GenerationVariant[];
    20→  /** Generation ID used for variant fetching (for mark-all-viewed) */
    21→  variantFetchGenerationId: string | null;
    22→}
    23→
    24→interface UseLightboxVariantBadgesReturn {
    25→  pendingTaskCount: number;
    26→  unviewedVariantCount: number;
    27→  handleMarkAllViewed: () => void;
    28→}
    29→
    30→export function useLightboxVariantBadges({
    31→  pendingTaskGenerationId,
    32→  selectedProjectId,
    33→  variants,
    34→  variantFetchGenerationId,
    35→}: UseLightboxVariantBadgesInput): UseLightboxVariantBadgesReturn {
    36→  const { pendingCount: pendingTaskCount } = usePendingGenerationTasks(pendingTaskGenerationId, selectedProjectId);
    37→
    38→  const unviewedVariantCount = useMemo(() => {
    39→    if (!variants || variants.length === 0) return 0;
    40→    return variants.filter((v) => v.viewed_at === null).length;
    41→  }, [variants]);
    42→
    43→  const { markAllViewed: markAllViewedMutation } = useMarkVariantViewed();
    44→  const handleMarkAllViewed = useCallback(() => {
    45→    if (variantFetchGenerationId) {
    46→      markAllViewedMutation(variantFetchGenerationId);
    47→    }
    48→  }, [markAllViewedMutation, variantFetchGenerationId]);
    49→
    50→  return {
    51→    pendingTaskCount,
    52→    unviewedVariantCount,
    53→    handleMarkAllViewed,
    54→  };
    55→}
    56→

1→/**
     2→ * usePendingGenerationTasks Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Tracks tasks that are "Queued" or "In Progress" that will create
     5→ * variants or derived generations from a given source generation.
     6→ *
     7→ * Works for both images and videos - checks task params for source generation references.
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import { useMemo } from 'react';
    11→import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    12→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
    13→import { TASK_STATUS } from '@/types/tasks';
    14→import { taskQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/tasks';
    15→
    16→interface PendingGenerationTask {
    17→  id: string;
    18→  status: string;
    19→  task_type: string;
    20→}
    21→
    22→interface UsePendingGenerationTasksReturn {
    23→  /** Number of pending tasks that will create variants/derived from this generation */
    24→  pendingCount: number;
    25→  /** The pending tasks */
    26→  pendingTasks: PendingGenerationTask[];
    27→  /** Loading state */
    28→  isLoading: boolean;
    29→}
    30→
    31→/**
    32→ * Check if task params reference a specific generation ID as a source.
    33→ * Checks common param fields where source generation is stored.
    34→ */
    35→function taskReferencesGeneration(params: Record<string, unknown> | null, generationId: string): boolean {
    36→  if (!params || !generationId) return false;
    37→
    38→  // Direct source references
    39→  if (params.based_on === generationId) return true;
    40→  if (params.source_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    41→  if (params.generation_id === generationId) return true;
    42→  if (params.input_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    43→  if (params.parent_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    44→  // Video segment tasks use pair_shot_generation_id
    45→  if (params.pair_shot_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    46→
    47→  // Check nested in orchestrator_details
    48→  const orchDetails = params.orchestrator_details as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    49→  if (orchDetails) {
    50→    if (orchDetails.based_on === generationId) return true;
    51→    if (orchDetails.source_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    52→    if (orchDetails.parent_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    53→    // Check pair_shot_generation_ids array
    54→    if (Array.isArray(orchDetails.pair_shot_generation_ids)) {
    55→      if (orchDetails.pair_shot_generation_ids.includes(generationId)) return true;
    56→    }
    57→  }
    58→
    59→  // Check full_orchestrator_payload
    60→  const fullPayload = params.full_orchestrator_payload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    61→  if (fullPayload) {
    62→    if (fullPayload.based_on === generationId) return true;
    63→    if (fullPayload.source_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    64→    if (fullPayload.parent_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    65→  }
    66→
    67→  // Check individual_segment_params (used by individual_travel_segment)
    68→  const individualParams = params.individual_segment_params as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    69→  if (individualParams) {
    70→    if (individualParams.pair_shot_generation_id === generationId) return true;
    71→  }
    72→
    73→  return false;
    74→}
    75→
    76→export function usePendingGenerationTasks(
    77→  generationId: string | null | undefined,
    78→  projectId: string | null | undefined
    79→): UsePendingGenerationTasksReturn {
    80→  // Query pending tasks for this project
    81→  const { data: pendingTasks, isLoading } = useQuery({
    82→    queryKey: [...taskQueryKeys.pendingGeneration(generationId!), projectId],
    83→    queryFn: async () => {
    84→      if (!generationId || !projectId) return [];
    85→
    86→      // Query tasks that are Queued or In Progress
    87→      const { data, error } = await supabase().from('tasks')
    88→        .select('id, status, task_type, params')
    89→        .eq('project_id', projectId)
    90→        .in('status', [TASK_STATUS.QUEUED, TASK_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS]);
    91→
    92→      if (error) {
    93→        console.error('[usePendingGenerationTasks] Query error:', error);
    94→        return [];
    95→      }
    96→
    97→      // Filter to tasks that reference this generation
    98→      const matchingTasks: PendingGenerationTask[] = (data || [])
    99→        .filter(task => taskReferencesGeneration(task.params as Record<string, unknown> | null, generationId))
   100→        .map(task => ({
   101→          id: task.id,
   102→          status: task.status,
   103→          task_type: task.task_type,
   104→        }));
   105→
   106→      return matchingTasks;
   107→    },
   108→    enabled: !!generationId && !!projectId,
   109→    // Poll to catch status changes
   110→    refetchInterval: 5000,
   111→    // Mark as stale immediately so invalidations trigger refetch
   112→    staleTime: 0,
   113→    // Short cache time - don't keep stale data around
   114→    gcTime: 10000,
   115→    // Always refetch when window regains focus
   116→    refetchOnWindowFocus: 'always',
   117→  });
   118→
   119→  const result = useMemo(() => ({
   120→    pendingCount: pendingTasks?.length || 0,
   121→    pendingTasks: pendingTasks || [],
   122→    isLoading,
   123→  }), [pendingTasks, isLoading]);
   124→
   125→  return result;
   126→}
   127→
   128→// NOTE: Default export removed - use named export { usePendingGenerationTasks } instead
   129→
```

> AGENT

Perfect! Now I found the root cause. Let me examine the query on line 87-90 more closely. The query filters tasks by:

```typescript
.in('status', [TASK_STATUS.QUEUED, TASK_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS])
```

This means it **only counts tasks with status "Queued" or "In Progress"**. If a task fails, its status changes to something else (likely "Failed"), so it's no longer included in this filter. Let me verify what the task statuses are:

    96→function useImageLightboxEnvironment(props: ImageLightboxProps) {
    97→  const { media, shotId, tasksPaneOpen, tasksPaneWidth } = props;
    98→
    99→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();

   129→
   130→  const [imageDimensions, setImageDimensions] = useState<{ width: number; height: number } | null>(() => {

   141→  const upscaleHook = useUpscale({ media, selectedProjectId, isVideo: false, shotId });

   143→  const editSettingsPersistence = useEditSettingsPersistence({

   145→    projectId: selectedProjectId,

   149→  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
