    45→
    46→/**
    47→ * Filter reference settings based on the selected reference mode.
    48→ * This ensures only relevant settings are passed to the backend based on what mode is active.
    49→ */
    50→function filterReferenceSettingsByMode(
    51→  referenceMode: 'style' | 'subject' | 'style-character' | 'scene' | 'custom' | undefined,
    52→  settings: {
    53→    style_reference_strength?: number;
    54→    subject_strength?: number;
    55→    subject_description?: string;
    56→    in_this_scene?: boolean;
    57→    in_this_scene_strength?: number;
    58→  }
    59→): Partial<typeof settings> {
    60→  // If no mode specified or custom mode, pass all settings as-is
    61→  if (!referenceMode || referenceMode === 'custom') {
    62→    return settings;
    63→  }
    64→
    65→  const filtered: Partial<typeof settings> = {};
    66→  
    67→  switch (referenceMode) {
    68→    case 'style':
    69→      // Style mode: only pass style strength, exclude subject and scene
    70→      if (settings.style_reference_strength !== undefined) {
    71→        filtered.style_reference_strength = settings.style_reference_strength;
    72→      }
    73→      break;
    74→
    75→    case 'subject':
    76→      // Subject mode: style at 1.1, subject at 0.5, plus description
    77→      filtered.style_reference_strength = 1.1;
    78→      filtered.subject_strength = 0.5;
    79→      if (settings.subject_description !== undefined && settings.subject_description.trim()) {
    80→        filtered.subject_description = settings.subject_description;
    81→      }
    82→      break;
    83→
    84→    case 'style-character':
    85→      // Style + Subject mode: pass both style and subject, exclude scene
    86→      if (settings.style_reference_strength !== undefined) {
    87→        filtered.style_reference_strength = settings.style_reference_strength;
    88→      }
    89→      if (settings.subject_strength !== undefined) {
    90→        filtered.subject_strength = settings.subject_strength;
    91→      }
    92→      if (settings.subject_description !== undefined && settings.subject_description.trim()) {
    93→        filtered.subject_description = settings.subject_description;
    94→      }
    95→      break;
    96→
    97→    case 'scene':
    98→      // Scene mode: style at 1.1, scene strength at 0.5
    99→      filtered.style_reference_strength = 1.1;
   100→      filtered.in_this_scene = true;
   101→      filtered.in_this_scene_strength = 0.5;
   102→      break;
   103→  }
   104→  
   105→  return filtered;
   106→}

   151→/**
   152→ * Validates an array of LoRA configs.
   153→ * Shared by single and batch image generation validation.
   154→ * @throws TaskValidationError if any LoRA is invalid
   155→ */
   156→function validateLoras(loras: PathLoraConfig[] | undefined): void {
   157→  try {
   158→    validateLoraConfigs(loras, {
   159→      pathField: 'path',
   160→      strengthField: 'strength',
   161→      strengthLabel: 'strength',
   162→      min: 0,
   163→      max: 2,
   164→    });
   165→  } catch (error) {
   166→    const maybeError = error as { field?: string; message?: string };
   167→    if (
   168→      typeof maybeError.field === 'string'
   169→      && maybeError.field.endsWith('.strength')
   170→      && typeof maybeError.message === 'string'
   171→      && maybeError.message.includes('must be between 0 and 2')
   172→    ) {
   173→      throw new TaskValidationError(
   174→        maybeError.message.replace('must be between', 'must be a number between'),
   175→        maybeError.field,
   176→      );
   177→    }
   178→    throw error;
   179→  }
   180→}
   181→
   182→/**
   183→ * Validates image generation task parameters
   184→ * @param params - Parameters to validate
   185→ * @throws TaskValidationError if validation fails
   186→ */
   187→function validateImageGenerationParams(params: ImageGenerationTaskParams): void {
   188→  validateRequiredFields(params, ['project_id', 'prompt']);
   189→
   190→  // Additional validation specific to image generation
   191→  validateNonEmptyString(params.prompt, 'prompt', 'Prompt');
   192→  validateSeed32Bit(params.seed);

   199→ * @param params - Parameters to validate

   201→ */
   202→function validateBatchImageGenerationParams(params: BatchImageGenerationTaskParams): void {
   203→  validateRequiredFields(params, ['project_id', 'prompts', 'imagesPerPrompt']);
   204→
   205→  if (params.prompts.length === 0) {
   206→    throw new TaskValidationError('At least one prompt is required', 'prompts');
   207→  }
   208→
   209→  if (params.imagesPerPrompt < 1 || params.imagesPerPrompt > 16) {
   210→    throw new TaskValidationError('Images per prompt must be between 1 and 16', 'imagesPerPrompt');
   211→  }
   212→
   213→  params.prompts.forEach((prompt, index) => {
   214→    if (!prompt.fullPrompt || prompt.fullPrompt.trim() === '') {
   215→      throw new TaskValidationError(`Prompt ${index + 1}: fullPrompt cannot be empty`, `prompts[${index}].fullPrompt`);
   216→    }
   217→  });
   218→
   219→  validateLoras(params.loras);
   220→}

   224→/**
   225→ * Options for calculateTaskResolution
   226→ */
   227→interface CalculateTaskResolutionOptions {
   228→  /** Project ID for resolution lookup */
   229→  projectId: string;
   230→  /** Optional explicit resolution override (bypasses all calculations) */
   231→  customResolution?: string;
   232→  /** Model name (determines if scaling is applied) */
   233→  modelName?: string;
   234→  /** User-configurable resolution scale multiplier (1.0-2.5x). Defaults to 1.5 for image models. */
   235→  resolution_scale?: number;
   236→  /** Resolution mode: 'project' uses project dimensions, 'custom' uses custom_aspect_ratio */
   237→  resolution_mode?: 'project' | 'custom';
   238→  /** Custom aspect ratio when resolution_mode is 'custom' (e.g., "16:9") */
   239→  custom_aspect_ratio?: string;
   240→}
   241→
   242→/**
   243→ * Calculates the final resolution for image generation tasks.
   244→ * Applies user-configurable scaling for supported image generation models.
   245→ * (internal use only - not exported)
   246→ * @param options - Resolution calculation options
   247→ * @returns Promise resolving to the final resolution string
   248→ */
   249→async function calculateTaskResolution(
   250→  options: CalculateTaskResolutionOptions
   251→): Promise<string> {
   252→  const { projectId, resolution_scale, resolution_mode, custom_aspect_ratio } = options;
   253→
   254→  // 1. If explicit custom resolution is provided, use it as-is (assumes it's already final)
   255→  if (options.customResolution?.trim()) {
   256→    return options.customResolution.trim();
   257→  }
   258→
   259→  // 2. Determine base resolution
   260→  let baseResolution: string;
   261→
   262→  if (resolution_mode === 'custom' && custom_aspect_ratio) {
   263→    // Use custom aspect ratio
   264→    baseResolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[custom_aspect_ratio] ?? '902x508';
   265→  } else {
   266→    // Use project resolution
   267→    const { resolution } = await resolveProjectResolution(projectId);
   268→    baseResolution = resolution;
   269→  }
   270→
   271→  // 3. Apply scaling for image generation models
   272→  const isImageGenerationModel = options.modelName === 'qwen-image' || options.modelName === 'qwen-image-2512' || options.modelName === 'z-image';
   273→  if (isImageGenerationModel) {
   274→    const scale = resolution_scale ?? 1.5; // Default to 1.5 if not specified
   275→    const [width, height] = baseResolution.split('x').map(Number);
   276→    const scaledWidth = Math.round(width * scale);
   277→    const scaledHeight = Math.round(height * scale);
   278→    const scaledResolution = `${scaledWidth}x${scaledHeight}`;
   279→    return scaledResolution;
   280→  }
   281→
   282→  return baseResolution;
   283→}
   284→
   285→const IN_SCENE_LORA_URL = 'https://huggingface.co/peteromallet/random_junk/resolve/main/in_scene_different_object_000010500.safetensors';
   286→
   287→function buildLorasParam(
   288→  loras: PathLoraConfig[] | undefined,
   289→  inSceneLora?: { url: string; strength: number },
   290→): { additional_loras?: Record<string, number> } {
   291→  const lorasMap: Record<string, number> = mapPathLorasToStrengthRecord(loras);
   292→
   293→  if (inSceneLora && inSceneLora.strength > 0) {
   294→    lorasMap[inSceneLora.url] = inSceneLora.strength;
   295→  }
   296→
   297→  return Object.keys(lorasMap).length > 0 ? { additional_loras: lorasMap } : {};
   298→}
   299→
   300→function buildReferenceParams(
   301→  styleReferenceImage: string | undefined,
   302→  mode: ReferenceMode | undefined,
   303→  settings: {
   304→    subjectReferenceImage?: string;
   305→    styleReferenceStrength?: number;
   306→    subjectStrength?: number;
   307→    subjectDescription?: string;
   308→    inThisScene?: boolean;
   309→    inThisSceneStrength?: number;
   310→  },
   311→): Record<string, unknown> {
   312→  if (!styleReferenceImage) return {};
   313→
   314→  const filteredSettings = filterReferenceSettingsByMode(mode, {
   315→    style_reference_strength: settings.styleReferenceStrength,
   316→    subject_strength: settings.subjectStrength,
   317→    subject_description: settings.subjectDescription,
   318→    in_this_scene: settings.inThisScene,
   319→    in_this_scene_strength: settings.inThisSceneStrength,
   320→  });
   321→
   322→  return {
   323→    style_reference_image: styleReferenceImage,
   324→    subject_reference_image: settings.subjectReferenceImage || styleReferenceImage,
   325→    ...filteredSettings,
   326→    ...composeOptionalFields([
   327→      {
   328→        [REDACTED],
   329→        value: filteredSettings.in_this_scene_strength,
   330→      },

   334→
   335→function buildHiresOverride(
   336→  hiresScale: number | undefined,
   337→  hiresDenoise: number | undefined,
   338→  hiresSteps: number | undefined,
   339→  additionalLoras: Record<string, number> | undefined,
   340→  baseLoras: Record<string, number> = {},
   341→): Record<string, unknown> {
   342→  return {
   343→    ...composeOptionalFields([
   344→      { key: 'hires_scale', value: hiresScale },
   345→      { key: 'hires_steps', value: hiresSteps },
   346→      { key: 'hires_denoise', value: hiresDenoise },
   347→    ]),
   348→    ...(additionalLoras && Object.keys(additionalLoras).length > 0
   349→      ? { additional_loras: { ...baseLoras, ...additionalLoras } }
   350→      : {}),

   354→function buildImageGenerationBaseParams(
   355→  params: ImageGenerationTaskParams,
   356→  taskId: string,
   357→  finalResolution: string,
   358→): Record<string, unknown> {
   359→  const modelName = resolveByPrecedence(params.model_name, "optimised-t2i") ?? "optimised-t2i";
   360→  const seed = resolveByPrecedence(params.seed, 11111) ?? 11111;
   361→  const steps = resolveByPrecedence(params.steps, 12) ?? 12;
   362→
   363→  return {
   364→    task_id: taskId,
   365→    model: modelName,
   366→    prompt: params.prompt,
   367→    resolution: finalResolution,
   368→    seed,
   369→    steps,
   370→    add_in_position: false,
   371→    ...composeOptionalFields([
   372→      {
   373→        key: 'negative_prompt',
   374→        value: params.negative_prompt,
   375→        include: isNonEmptyString,
   376→      },
   377→    ]),
   378→  };
   379→}
   380→
   381→/**
   382→ * Creates a single image generation task using the unified approach
   383→ * This replaces the direct call to the single-image-generate edge function
   384→ * (internal use only - used by createBatchImageGenerationTasks)
   385→ *
   386→ * @param params - Image generation task parameters
   387→ * @returns Promise resolving to the created task
   388→ */
   389→async function createImageGenerationTask(params: ImageGenerationTaskParams): Promise<TaskCreationResult> {
   390→  return runTaskCreationPipeline({
   391→    params,
   392→    context: 'ImageGeneration',
   393→    validate: validateImageGenerationParams,
   394→    buildTaskRequest: async (requestParams) => {
   395→      // 1. Calculate final resolution (handles Qwen scaling automatically)
   396→      const finalResolution = await calculateTaskResolution({
   397→        projectId: requestParams.project_id,
   398→        customResolution: requestParams.resolution,
   399→        modelName: requestParams.model_name,

    79→export function setThumbnailInParams(

    84→  const config = THUMBNAIL_PATH_CONFIG[taskType] || THUMBNAIL_PATH_CONFIG.default;

    86→
    87→  // Navigate to parent path and ensure it exists
    88→  let target = updatedParams;
    89→  for (let i = 0; i < config.path.length - 1; i++) {
    90→    const key = config.path[i];
    91→    if (!target[key]) {
    92→      target[key] = {};
    93→    }
    94→    target = target[key];
    95→  }
    96→
    97→  // Set the thumbnail URL
    98→  const finalKey = config.path[config.path.length - 1];
    99→  target[finalKey] = thumbnailUrl;

## Summary: task_type Usage Analysis

I've completed a thorough search of the codebase for places where `task_type` is used as free text rather than matching a fixed set of known values. Here are my findings:

### Critical Issues Found

**1. Dynamic task_type Construction in Travel Between Images** 
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/tasks/travelBetweenImages/createTravelBetweenImagesTask.ts` (line 56)
- **Code**: `const taskType = isTurboMode ? 'wan_2_2_i2v' : 'travel_orchestrator';`
- **Issue**: Task type is determined at runtime based on the `turbo_mode` parameter. While the values are hardcoded strings, the pattern is dynamic.

**2. Dynamic task_type Construction in Image Generation** 
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/tasks/imageGeneration.ts` (lines 403-419)
- **Code**: A switch statement that maps model names to task types:
  ```typescript
  const taskType = (() => {
    const modelName = requestParams.model_name;
    const hasStyleRef = !!requestParams.style_reference_image;
    switch (modelName) {
      case 'qwen-image':
        return hasStyleRef ? 'qwen_image_style' : 'qwen_image';
      case 'qwen-image-2512':
        return 'qwen_image_2512';
      case 'z-image':
        return 'z_image_turbo';
      default:
        return 'wan_2_2_t2i';  // FALLBACK
    }
  })();
  ```
- **Issue**: Task type is computed at runtime based on `model_name` parameter. All code paths are hardcoded strings, but the **default fallback could cause issues** if new models are added without updating this switch statement.

### No Validation in create-task Edge Function

- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/create-task/request.ts`
- **Finding**: The `parseCreateTaskBody()` function validates that `task_type` is:
  - Non-null
  - Non-empty string
  - **NOT checked against any allowed values**
- **Impact**: The server accepts ANY non-empty string as a task_type. If a FK constraint is added to `tasks.task_type` → `task_types.name` without prior validation, the create-task function could accept clients sending unknown task_type values that would fail at the database layer.

### Task Types Actually Used

**Actively Created (Frontend)**:
- `z_image_turbo_i2i` - conditional in imageGeneration.ts
- `image_inpaint`
- `image-upscale`
- `annotated_image_edit`
- `animate_character`
- `edit_video_orchestrator`
- `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_style`, `qwen_image_2512` - all conditional in imageGeneration.ts
- `z_image_turbo` - conditional fallback in imageGeneration.ts
- `wan_2_2_t2i` - fallback in imageGeneration.ts
- `travel_orchestrator` - conditional in travelBetweenImages.ts
- `wan_2_2_i2v` - conditional in travelBetweenImages.ts

**Actively Used (Edge Functions & Backend)**:
- `travel_segment`
- `travel_stitch`
- `join_clips_orchestrator`
- `join_clips_segment`
- `join_final_stitch`
- `edit_video_segment`
- `video_enhance`
- `single_image`

**Legacy/Hidden Task Types** (marked `isVisible: false` in config):
- `extract_frame`
- `generate_openpose`
- `rife_interpolate_images`
- `wgp`
- `edit_travel_kontext`
- `edit_travel_flux`

### Additional Findings

**No UPDATE operations on task_type**: The codebase contains no UPDATE statements that modify `task_type` after creation. This is good design—task types are immutable.

**No direct RPC/SQL task INSERTs**: All task creation goes through the `create-task` edge function. There are no database trigger inserts or RPC functions that bypass this validation point.

**Discord stats function**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/discord-daily-stats/index.ts` (lines 68, 73) contains hardcoded lists of task types for filtering statistics, which would need to be updated if task_types are constrained.

### Risk Assessment for FK Constraint

**HIGH RISK** if FK constraint is added without accompanying changes:

1. **create-task accepts any value**: Clients can send unknown task_type strings that will fail at the DB layer
2. **No pre-validation**: The edge function doesn't validate against `task_types` table
3. **Dynamic construction paths**: Frontend code uses conditionals and switches that could create unexpected task_type values if logic changes or defaults are invoked
4. **Fallback mechanism**: The `wan_2_2_t2i` fallback in imageGeneration.ts could silently create tasks with this type if a model isn't matched in the switch statement

### Recommendations Before Adding FK Constraint

1. **Add validation to create-task**: Validate `task_type` against the `task_types` table in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/create-task/request.ts`
2. **Audit and lock the switch statements**: The two dynamic construction sites should have exhaustive case coverage or be redesigned to avoid fallbacks
3. **Ensure all used task_type values exist in task_types**: Verify that all 20+ distinct task_type values currently created are present in the DB before adding the constraint
4. **Update discord-daily-stats**: If the hardcoded task_type lists in this function become stale, update them to match the task_types table

   101→        if (rawError) throw rawError;
   102→        if (!page || page.length === 0) break;
   103→        allTasks.push(...page);
   104→        if (page.length < PAGE_SIZE) break;
   105→        offset += PAGE_SIZE;
   106→      }
   107→
   108→      // Aggregate in memory
   109→      const dayMap = new Map<string, DayBucket>();
   110→      for (const task of allTasks) {
   111→        const date = task.created_at.substring(0, 10); // YYYY-MM-DD
   112→        if (!dayMap.has(date)) {
   113→          dayMap.set(date, { date, images_generated: 0, images_edited: 0, videos_generated: 0 });
   114→        }
   115→        const bucket = dayMap.get(date)!;
   116→        const name = task.task_type;
   117→
   118→        if (IMAGE_GENERATED_TYPES.has(name)) {
   119→          bucket.images_generated++;
   120→        } else if (IMAGE_EDITED_TYPES.has(name)) {
   121→          bucket.images_edited++;
   122→        } else if (VIDEO_GENERATED_TYPES.has(name)) {
   123→          bucket.videos_generated++;
   124→        }
   125→      }
   126→
   127→      buckets = Array.from(dayMap.values()).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
   128→    } else {
   129→      buckets = dailyStats;
   130→    }
   131→
   132→    if (buckets.length === 0) {
   133→      logger.info("No stats data found");
   134→      await logger.flush();
   135→      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ message: "No data" }), {
   136→        status: 200,
   137→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   138→      });
   139→    }
   140→
   141→    // Determine if we should aggregate by week (>90 days of data)
   142→    const shouldAggregateWeekly = buckets.length > 90;
   143→    const periodData = shouldAggregateWeekly ? aggregateByWeek(buckets) : buckets;
   144→
   145→    // Convert to cumulative
   146→    const chartData: DayBucket[] = [];
   147→    let cumImg = 0, cumEdit = 0, cumVid = 0;
   148→    for (const b of periodData) {
   149→      cumImg += b.images_generated;
   150→      cumEdit += b.images_edited;
   151→      cumVid += b.videos_generated;
   152→      chartData.push({ date: b.date, images_generated: cumImg, images_edited: cumEdit, videos_generated: cumVid });
   153→    }
   154→
   155→    // Calculate yesterday's stats
   156→    const yesterday = new Date();
   157→    yesterday.setUTCDate(yesterday.getUTCDate() - 1);
   158→    const yesterdayStr = yesterday.toISOString().substring(0, 10);
   159→    const yesterdayStats = buckets.find((b) => b.date === yesterdayStr) || {
   160→      date: yesterdayStr,
   161→      images_generated: 0,
   162→      images_edited: 0,
   163→      videos_generated: 0,
   164→    };
   165→
   166→    // Calculate all-time totals
   167→    const totals = buckets.reduce(
   168→      (acc, b) => ({
   169→        images_generated: acc.images_generated + b.images_generated,
   170→        images_edited: acc.images_edited + b.images_edited,
   171→        videos_generated: acc.videos_generated + b.videos_generated,
   172→      }),
   173→      { images_generated: 0, images_edited: 0, videos_generated: 0 }
   174→    );
   175→
   176→    // Generate chart via QuickChart.io
   177→    const chartUrl = await generateChart(chartData, shouldAggregateWeekly);
   178→
   179→    // Format date for title
   180→    const titleDate = yesterday.toLocaleDateString("en-US", {
   181→      month: "short",
   182→      day: "numeric",
   183→      year: "numeric",
   184→      timeZone: "UTC",
   185→    });
   186→
   187→    // Post to Discord
   188→    const embed = {
   189→      title: `Daily Stats — ${titleDate}`,
   190→      color: 0x6366f1, // indigo
   191→      fields: [
   192→        {
   193→          name: "Images Generated",
   194→          value: `**${yesterdayStats.images_generated.toLocaleString()}**`,
   195→          inline: true,
   196→        },
   197→        {
   198→          name: "Images Edited",
   199→          value: `**${yesterdayStats.images_edited.toLocaleString()}**`,
   200→          inline: true,
   201→        },
   202→        {
   203→          name: "Videos Generated",
   204→          value: `**${yesterdayStats.videos_generated.toLocaleString()}**`,
   205→          inline: true,
   206→        },
   207→      ],
   208→      image: chartUrl ? { url: chartUrl } : undefined,
   209→      footer: {
   210→        text: `All time: ${totals.images_generated.toLocaleString()} imgs generated · ${totals.images_edited.toLocaleString()} imgs edited · ${totals.videos_generated.toLocaleString()} videos`,
   211→      },
   212→      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   213→    };
   214→
   215→    const discordPayload = { embeds: [embed] };
   216→
   217→    const discordRes = await fetch(discordWebhookUrl, {
   218→      method: "POST",
   219→      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   220→      body: JSON.stringify(discordPayload),
   221→    });
   222→
   223→    if (!discordRes.ok) {
   224→      const errText = await discordRes.text();
   225→      throw new Error(`Discord webhook failed (${discordRes.status}): ${errText}`);
   226→    }
   227→
   228→    logger.info("Discord message sent successfully", {
   229→      yesterday_images_gen: yesterdayStats.images_generated,
   230→      yesterday_images_edit: yesterdayStats.images_edited,
   231→      yesterday_videos: yesterdayStats.videos_generated,
   232→      chart_url: chartUrl,
   233→    });
   234→    await logger.flush();
   235→
   236→    return new Response(
   237→      JSON.stringify({
   238→        success: true,
   239→        yesterday: yesterdayStats,
   240→        totals,
   241→        chart_url: chartUrl,
   242→      }),
   243→      { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
   244→    );
   245→  } catch (error) {
   246→    logger.error("Failed to generate/send daily stats", { error: error.message });
   247→    await logger.flush();
   248→    return new Response(`Error: ${error.message}`, { status: 500 });
   249→  }
   250→});
   251→
   252→/**
   253→ * Aggregate daily buckets into weekly buckets (Mon-Sun weeks)
   254→ */
   255→function aggregateByWeek(buckets: DayBucket[]): DayBucket[] {
   256→  const weekMap = new Map<string, DayBucket>();
   257→
   258→  for (const bucket of buckets) {
   259→    const date = new Date(bucket.date + "T00:00:00Z");
   260→    // Get Monday of this week
   261→    const day = date.getUTCDay();
   262→    const diff = day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day; // Monday = 1
   263→    const monday = new Date(date);
   264→    monday.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + diff);
   265→    const [REDACTED]().substring(0, 10);
   266→
   267→    if (!weekMap.has(weekKey)) {
   268→      weekMap.set(weekKey, { date: weekKey, images_generated: 0, images_edited: 0, videos_generated: 0 });
   269→    }
   270→    const week = weekMap.get(weekKey)!;
   271→    week.images_generated += bucket.images_generated;
   272→    week.images_edited += bucket.images_edited;
   273→    week.videos_generated += bucket.videos_generated;
   274→  }
   275→
   276→  return Array.from(weekMap.values()).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
   277→}
   278→
   279→/**
   280→ * Generate a chart image URL via QuickChart.io
   281→ */
   282→async function generateChart(
   283→  data: DayBucket[],
   284→  _isWeekly: boolean
   285→): Promise<string | null> {
   286→  const labels = data.map((d) => {
   287→    const date = new Date(d.date + "T00:00:00Z");
   288→    return date.toLocaleDateString("en-US", {
   289→      month: "short",
   290→      day: "numeric",
   291→      timeZone: "UTC",
   292→    });
   293→  });
   294→
   295→  const chartConfig = {
   296→    type: "line",
   297→    data: {
   298→      labels,
   299→      datasets: [
   300→        {
   301→          label: "Images Generated",
   302→          data: data.map((d) => d.images_generated),
   303→          borderColor: COLORS.imagesGenerated.border,
   304→          backgroundColor: COLORS.imagesGenerated.bg.replace("0.85", "0.15"),
   305→          borderWidth: 2,
   306→          fill: true,
   307→          tension: 0.3,
   308→          pointRadius: 0,
   309→        },
   310→        {
   311→          label: "Images Edited",
   312→          data: data.map((d) => d.images_edited),
   313→          borderColor: COLORS.imagesEdited.border,
   314→          backgroundColor: COLORS.imagesEdited.bg.replace("0.85", "0.15"),
   315→          borderWidth: 2,
   316→          fill: true,
   317→          tension: 0.3,
   318→          pointRadius: 0,
   319→        },
   320→        {
   321→          label: "Videos Generated",
   322→          data: data.map((d) => d.videos_generated),
   323→          borderColor: COLORS.videosGenerated.border,
   324→          backgroundColor: COLORS.videosGenerated.bg.replace("0.85", "0.15"),
   325→          borderWidth: 2,
   326→          fill: true,
   327→          tension: 0.3,
   328→          pointRadius: 0,
   329→        },
   330→      ],
   331→    },
   332→    options: {
   333→      plugins: {
   334→        title: {
   335→          display: false,
   336→        },
   337→        legend: {
   338→          position: "bottom",
   339→          labels: {
   340→            color: "#cbd5e1",
   341→            padding: 16,
   342→            usePointStyle: true,
   343→            pointStyle: "circle",
   344→            font: { size: 11 },
   345→          },
   346→        },
   347→      },
   348→      scales: {
   349→        x: {
   350→          ticks: { color: "#64748b", maxRotation: 45, font: { size: 10 } },
   351→          grid: { display: false },
   352→        },
   353→        y: {
   354→          ticks: { color: "#64748b", font: { size: 10 } },
   355→          grid: { color: "rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.08)" },
   356→          beginAtZero: true,
   357→        },
   358→      },
   359→    },
   360→  };
   361→
   362→  try {
   363→    const res = await fetch("https://quickchart.io/chart/create", {
   364→      method: "POST",
   365→      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   366→      body: JSON.stringify({
   367→        backgroundColor: "#1a1a2e",
   368→        width: 800,
   369→        height: 350,
   370→        format: "png",
   371→        chart: chartConfig,
   372→      }),
   373→    });
   374→
   375→    if (!res.ok) {
   376→      console.error(`${LOG_PREFIX} QuickChart error: ${res.status}`);
   377→      return null;
   378→    }
   379→
   380→    const result = await res.json();
   381→    return result.url || null;
   382→  } catch (err) {
   383→    console.error(`${LOG_PREFIX} QuickChart request failed:`, err);
   384→    return null;
   385→  }
   386→}
   387→

     9→/**

   122→      // ESSENTIAL: Get counts from RPC functions (4 parallel calls)

   283→        .map(task => ({
   284→          task_id: task.id,
   285→          task_type: task.task_type,
   286→          user_id: task.projects.user_id,
   287→          created_at: task.created_at
   288→        }));
   289→
   290→      // Filter active tasks (orchestrator, credits, run_type, api-worker exclusion)
   291→      const active_tasks = (activeResult.data || [])
   292→        .filter(task => {
   293→          if (task.task_type?.toLowerCase().includes('orchestrator')) return false;
   294→          if (task.projects.users.credits <= 0) return false;
   295→          if (runType === 'gpu' && task.worker_id === 'api-worker-main') return false;
   296→          if (runType && taskTypeRunTypeMap) {
   297→            const taskRunType = taskTypeRunTypeMap.get(task.task_type);
   298→            if (!taskRunType || taskRunType !== runType) return false;
   299→          }
   300→          return true;
   301→        })
   302→        .map(task => ({
   303→          task_id: task.id,
   304→          task_type: task.task_type,
   305→          worker_id: task.worker_id,
   306→          user_id: task.projects.user_id,
   307→          started_at: task.updated_at
   308→        }));
   309→
   310→      // Debug logging (only when debug=true)
   311→      if (debug) {
   312→        logger.debug('Service role counts', {
   313→          runType: runType || 'ALL',
   314→          queued_only, blocked_by_capacity, blocked_by_deps, blocked_by_settings,
   315→          potentially_claimable, active_only,
   316→          queued_tasks_count: queued_tasks.length,
   317→          active_tasks_count: active_tasks.length,
   318→          users_with_tasks: user_stats.filter(u => u.in_progress_tasks > 0 || u.queued_tasks > 0).length,
   319→        });
   320→
   321→        // Validation warnings (array may differ due to 100-task limit or race conditions)
   322→        if (active_tasks.length !== active_only) {
   323→          logger.warn('active_tasks array mismatch', { array: active_tasks.length, count: active_only });
   324→        }
   325→      }
   326→
   327→      const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
   328→      if (debug) {
   329→        logger.debug('Completed', { elapsed_ms: elapsed });
   330→      }
   331→
   332→      return new Response(JSON.stringify({
   333→        mode: 'count',
   334→        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   335→        run_type_filter: runType,
   336→        totals: {
   337→          // Core counts (capacity-limited, for claim logic)
   338→          queued_only,              // Immediately claimable (capacity-limited)
   339→          active_only,              // Currently being processed
   340→          queued_plus_active,       // Total workload
   341→          // Breakdown for smarter scaling decisions
   342→          blocked_by_capacity,      // Will free up as tasks complete
   343→          blocked_by_deps,          // Waiting on dependencies (uncertain)
   344→          blocked_by_settings,      // User has cloud disabled (won't become claimable)
   345→          potentially_claimable     // queued_only + blocked_by_capacity (for scaling)
   346→        },
   347→        queued_tasks,
   348→        active_tasks,
   349→        users: user_stats,
   350→        elapsed_ms: debug ? elapsed : undefined
   351→      }), {
   352→        status: 200,
   353→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
   354→      });
   355→    } else {
   356→      // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   357→      // USER TOKEN PATH: Task statistics for specific user
   358→      // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   359→
   360→      // ESSENTIAL: Get counts, user info, and project IDs (4 parallel calls)
   361→      const [countQueuedOnly, countQueuedPlusActive, analysisResult, projectsResult] = await Promise.all([
   362→        supabaseAdmin.rpc('count_eligible_tasks_user_pat', { p_user_id: callerId, p_include_active: false }),
   363→        supabaseAdmin.rpc('count_eligible_tasks_user_pat', { p_user_id: callerId, p_include_active: true }),
   364→        supabaseAdmin.rpc('analyze_task_availability_user_pat', { p_user_id: callerId, p_include_active: true }),
   365→        supabaseAdmin.from('projects').select('id').eq('user_id', callerId)
   366→      ]);
   367→
   368→      if (countQueuedOnly.error) {
   369→        logger.error("User count (queued only) error", { error: countQueuedOnly.error.message, user_id: callerId });
   370→        throw countQueuedOnly.error;
   371→      }
   372→      if (countQueuedPlusActive.error) {
   373→        logger.error("User count (queued+active) error", { error: countQueuedPlusActive.error.message, user_id: callerId });
   374→        throw countQueuedPlusActive.error;
   375→      }
   376→
   377→      const queued_only = countQueuedOnly.data ?? 0;
   378→      const queued_plus_active = countQueuedPlusActive.data ?? 0;
   379→      const active_only = Math.max(0, queued_plus_active - queued_only);
   380→
   381→      const analysis = analysisResult.data || {};
   382→      const eligible_queued = analysis.eligible_count ?? 0;
   383→      const user_info = analysis.user_info ?? {};
   384→
   385→      const projectIds = projectsResult.data?.map(p => p.id) || [];
   386→
   387→      let queued_tasks: unknown[] = [];
   388→      let active_tasks: unknown[] = [];
   389→
   390→      if (projectIds.length > 0) {
   391→        // Fetch task details (parallel)
   392→        const [queuedResult, activeResult] = await Promise.all([
   393→          supabaseAdmin
   394→            .from('tasks')
   395→            .select('id, task_type, created_at, project_id')
   396→            .eq('status', 'Queued')
   397→            .in('project_id', projectIds)
   398→            .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
   399→            .limit(50),
   400→          supabaseAdmin
   401→            .from('tasks')
   402→            .select('id, task_type, worker_id, updated_at, project_id')
   403→            .eq('status', 'In Progress')
   404→            .in('project_id', projectIds)
   405→            .not('worker_id', 'is', null)
   406→            .order('updated_at', { ascending: true })
   407→            .limit(50)
   408→        ]);
   409→
   410→        // Filter and format queued tasks
   411→        queued_tasks = (queuedResult.data || [])
   412→          .filter(task => !task.task_type?.toLowerCase().includes('orchestrator'))
   413→          .map(task => ({
   414→            task_id: task.id,
   415→            task_type: task.task_type,
   416→            user_id: callerId,
   417→            created_at: task.created_at
   418→          }));
   419→
   420→        // Filter and format active tasks
   421→        active_tasks = (activeResult.data || [])
   422→          .filter(task => !task.task_type?.toLowerCase().includes('orchestrator'))
   423→          .map(task => ({
   424→            task_id: task.id,
   425→            task_type: task.task_type,
   426→            worker_id: task.worker_id,
   427→            user_id: callerId,
   428→            started_at: task.updated_at
   429→          }));
   430→      }
   431→
   432→      if (debug) {
   433→        logger.debug('User counts', { user_id: callerId, queued_only, active_only, eligible_queued });
   434→      }
   435→
   436→      const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
   437→
   438→      return new Response(JSON.stringify({
   439→        mode: 'count',
   440→        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   441→        user_id: callerId,
   442→        run_type_filter: runType,
   443→        totals: {
   444→          queued_only,
   445→          active_only,
   446→          queued_plus_active,
   447→          eligible_queued
   448→        },
   449→        queued_tasks,
   450→        active_tasks,
   451→        user_info,
   452→        debug_summary: {
   453→          at_capacity: active_only >= 5,
   454→          capacity_used_pct: Math.round((active_only / 5) * 100),
   455→          can_claim_more: active_only < 5 && eligible_queued > 0
   456→        },
   457→        elapsed_ms: debug ? elapsed : undefined
   458→      }), {
   459→        status: 200,
   460→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
   461→      });
   462→    }
   463→  } catch (error) {
   464→    logger.error("Unexpected error", { error: error.message });
   465→    await logger.flush();
   466→    return new Response(`Internal server error: ${error.message}`, { status: 500 });
   467→  }
   468→});

   211→// Pricing constants for video_enhance task (fal.ai rates)
   212→const VIDEO_ENHANCE_PRICING = {
   213→  // FILM (interpolation): $0.0013 per compute second
   214→  FILM_COST_PER_SECOND: 0.0013,
   215→  // FlashVSR (upscale): $0.0005 per megapixel (width × height × frames / 1,000,000)
   216→  FLASHVSR_COST_PER_MEGAPIXEL: 0.0005,
   217→};
   218→
   219→interface VideoEnhanceResult {
   220→  interpolation_compute_seconds?: number;
   221→  output_width?: number;
   222→  output_height?: number;
   223→  output_frames?: number;
   224→  result?: VideoEnhanceResult;
   225→}
   226→
   227→interface VideoEnhanceBreakdown {
   228→  interpolation?: { compute_seconds: number; cost_per_second: number; cost: number };
   229→  upscale?: { output_width: number; output_height: number; output_frames: number; megapixels: number; cost_per_megapixel: number; cost: number };
   230→}
   231→
   232→// Special cost calculation for video_enhance task
   233→// Combines FILM (time-based) and FlashVSR (megapixel-based) pricing
   234→function calculateVideoEnhanceCost(taskParams: VideoEnhanceResult): { cost: number; breakdown: VideoEnhanceBreakdown } {
   235→  let totalCost = 0;
   236→  const breakdown: VideoEnhanceBreakdown = {};
   237→
   238→  // Get result data from task params (worker should populate this)
   239→  const result = taskParams?.result || taskParams;
   240→
   241→  // FILM interpolation cost: $0.0013 per compute second
   242→  if (result?.interpolation_compute_seconds && result.interpolation_compute_seconds > 0) {
   243→    const filmCost = VIDEO_ENHANCE_PRICING.FILM_COST_PER_SECOND * result.interpolation_compute_seconds;
   244→    totalCost += filmCost;
   245→    breakdown.interpolation = {
   246→      compute_seconds: result.interpolation_compute_seconds,
   247→      cost_per_second: VIDEO_ENHANCE_PRICING.FILM_COST_PER_SECOND,
   248→      cost: Math.round(filmCost * 1000000) / 1000000,
   249→    };
   250→  }
   251→
   252→  // FlashVSR upscale cost: $0.0005 per megapixel
   253→  if (result?.output_width && result?.output_height && result?.output_frames) {
   254→    const megapixels = (result.output_width * result.output_height * result.output_frames) / 1_000_000;
   255→    const upscaleCost = VIDEO_ENHANCE_PRICING.FLASHVSR_COST_PER_MEGAPIXEL * megapixels;
   256→    totalCost += upscaleCost;
   257→    breakdown.upscale = {
   258→      output_width: result.output_width,
   259→      output_height: result.output_height,
   260→      output_frames: result.output_frames,
   261→      megapixels: Math.round(megapixels * 100) / 100,
   262→      cost_per_megapixel: VIDEO_ENHANCE_PRICING.FLASHVSR_COST_PER_MEGAPIXEL,
   263→      cost: Math.round(upscaleCost * 1000000) / 1000000,
   264→    };
   265→  }
   266→
   267→  return {
   268→    cost: Math.round(totalCost * 1000000) / 1000000,
   269→    breakdown,
   270→  };
   271→}
   272→
   273→interface CostFactors {
   274→  resolution?: Record<string, number>;
   275→  frameCount?: number;
   276→  modelType?: Record<string, number>;
   277→}
   278→
   279→interface TaskCostParams {
   280→  resolution?: string;
   281→  frame_count?: number;
   282→  model_type?: string;
   283→  result?: VideoEnhanceResult;
   284→  [key: string]: unknown;
   285→}
   286→
   287→// Calculate cost based on billing type and task configuration
   288→function calculateTaskCost(
   289→  taskType: string,
   290→  billingType: string,
   291→  baseCostPerSecond: number,
   292→  unitCost: number,
   293→  durationSeconds: number,
   294→  costFactors: CostFactors | null,
   295→  taskParams: TaskCostParams
   296→): { cost: number; breakdown?: VideoEnhanceBreakdown } {
   297→  // Special case: video_enhance has compound pricing (FILM + FlashVSR)
   298→  if (taskType === 'video_enhance') {
   299→    return calculateVideoEnhanceCost(taskParams);
   300→  }
   301→
   302→  let totalCost;
   303→  if (billingType === 'per_unit') {
   304→    totalCost = unitCost || 0;
   305→  } else {
   306→    totalCost = baseCostPerSecond * durationSeconds;
   307→  }
   308→
   309→  // Apply cost factors regardless of billing type
   310→  if (costFactors) {
   311→    if (costFactors.resolution && taskParams.resolution) {
   312→      const resolutionMultiplier = costFactors.resolution[taskParams.resolution] || 1;
   313→      totalCost *= resolutionMultiplier;
   314→    }
   315→    if (costFactors.frameCount && taskParams.frame_count) {
   316→      if (billingType === 'per_unit') {
   317→        totalCost += costFactors.frameCount * taskParams.frame_count;
   318→      } else {
   319→        totalCost += costFactors.frameCount * taskParams.frame_count * durationSeconds;
   320→      }
   321→    }
   322→    if (costFactors.modelType && taskParams.model_type) {
   323→      const modelMultiplier = costFactors.modelType[taskParams.model_type] || 1;
   324→      totalCost *= modelMultiplier;
   325→    }
   326→  }
   327→
   328→  return { cost: Math.round(totalCost * 1000) / 1000 };
   329→}

   331→serve(async (req) => {
   332→  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL');
   333→  const serviceKey = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY');
   334→

   336→    console.error("[CALCULATE-TASK-COST] Missing required environment variables");
   337→    return errorResponse('server_configuration_error', 'Server configuration error', 500);
   338→  }

   343→      persistSession: false

   348→  const logger = new SystemLogger(supabaseAdmin, 'calculate-task-cost');
   349→

   362→  const auth = await authenticateRequest(req, supabaseAdmin, "[CALCULATE-TASK-COST]");
   363→  if (!auth.success) {
   364→    logger.warn(auth.error || "Authentication failed");
   365→    await logger.flush();
   366→    return errorResponse('authentication_failed', auth.error || "Authentication failed", auth.statusCode || 401);
   367→  }

   369→  let requestBody: Record<string, unknown>;

   371→    const parsed = await req.json();

   373→      logger.warn("Invalid JSON body shape");
   374→      await logger.flush();
   375→      return errorResponse('invalid_json', 'Request body must be a JSON object', 400);
   376→    }
   377→    requestBody = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
   378→  } catch (jsonError) {
   379→    logger.warn("Invalid JSON body", {
   380→      error: jsonError instanceof Error ? jsonError.message : String(jsonError),
   381→    });
   382→    await logger.flush();
   383→    return errorResponse('invalid_json', 'Request body must be valid JSON', 400);
   384→  }
   385→
   386→  try {
   387→    const task_id = typeof requestBody.task_id === 'string' ? requestBody.task_id : undefined;
   388→
   389→    if (!task_id) {
   390→      logger.error("Missing task_id in request");
   391→      await logger.flush();
   392→      return errorResponse('missing_task_id', 'task_id is required', 400);
   393→    }
   394→
   395→    if (!auth.isServiceRole) {
   396→      if (!auth.userId) {
   397→        logger.warn("Missing authenticated user id for non-service request");
   398→        await logger.flush();
   399→        return errorResponse('authentication_failed', 'Authentication failed', 401);
   400→      }
   401→      const ownership = await verifyTaskOwnership(
   402→        supabaseAdmin,
   403→        task_id,
   404→        auth.userId,
   405→        "[CALCULATE-TASK-COST]",
   406→      );
   407→      if (!ownership.success) {
   408→        logger.warn("Task ownership verification failed", {
   409→          task_id,
   410→          user_id: auth.userId,
   411→          reason: ownership.error,
   412→        });
   413→        await logger.flush();
   414→        return errorResponse(
   415→          ownership.statusCode === 404 ? 'task_not_found' : 'forbidden',
   416→          ownership.error || "Forbidden",
   417→          ownership.statusCode || 403,
   418→        );
   419→      }
   420→    }
   421→
   422→    // Set task_id for all subsequent logs
   423→    logger.setDefaultTaskId(task_id);
   424→    logger.info("Calculating task cost", { task_id });
   425→
   426→    // Get task details with task_types joined via FK (eliminates separate task_types query)
   427→    const { data: task, error: taskError } = await supabaseAdmin
   428→      .from('tasks')
   429→      .select(`
   430→        id,
   431→        task_type,
   432→        params,
   433→        status,
   434→        generation_started_at,
   435→        generation_processed_at,
   436→        project_id,
   437→        projects(user_id),
   438→        task_types!tasks_task_type_fkey(id, billing_type, base_cost_per_second, unit_cost, cost_factors, is_active)
   439→      `)
   440→      .eq('id', task_id)
   441→      .single();
   442→
   443→    if (taskError) {
   444→      logger.error("Task not found", { error: taskError?.message });
   445→      await logger.flush();
   446→      return errorResponse('task_not_found', 'Task not found', 404);
   447→    }
   448→
   449→    const typedTaskResult = parseTaskWithProject(task);
   450→    if (!typedTaskResult.ok) {
   451→      logger.error("Task payload shape invalid", {
   452→        failure: typedTaskResult.failure,
   453→        task_id,
   454→      });
   455→      await logger.flush();
   456→      return errorResponse(
   457→        typedTaskResult.failure.errorCode,
   458→        typedTaskResult.failure.message,
   459→        422,
   460→      );
   461→    }
   462→    const typedTask = typedTaskResult.task;
   463→
   464→    logger.debug("Task found", {
   465→      task_type: typedTask.task_type,
   466→      status: typedTask.status,
   467→      has_timestamps: !!(typedTask.generation_started_at && typedTask.generation_processed_at)
   468→    });
   469→
   470→    // Check if task has both start and end times
   471→    if (!typedTask.generation_started_at || !typedTask.generation_processed_at) {
   472→      logger.error("Missing timestamps", {
   473→        has_started_at: !!typedTask.generation_started_at,
   474→        has_processed_at: !!typedTask.generation_processed_at
   475→      });
   476→      await logger.flush();
   477→      return errorResponse(
   478→        'missing_generation_timestamps',
   479→        'Task must have both generation_started_at and generation_processed_at timestamps',
   480→        400,
   481→      );
   482→    }
   483→
   484→    // Check if task is a sub-task of an orchestrator - skip billing if so (parent will be billed)
   485→    // Uses shared detection: checks all param paths, validates UUID, guards against self-reference
   486→    const subTaskOrchestratorId = getSubTaskOrchestratorId(typedTask.params, typedTask.id);
   487→
   488→    if (subTaskOrchestratorId) {
   489→      logger.info("Skipping cost calculation (sub-task)", {
   490→        orchestrator_task_id: subTaskOrchestratorId
   491→      });
   492→      await logger.flush();
   493→      return jsonResponse({
   494→        success: true,
   495→        skipped: true,
   496→        reason: 'Task is sub-task of orchestrator, parent task will be billed',
   497→        orchestrator_task_id: subTaskOrchestratorId,
   498→        task_id: typedTask.id
   499→      });
   500→    }
   501→
   502→    // Check if this is an orchestrator task - calculate cost based on sub-task durations
   503→    let subTasks: CompletedSubTaskRow[] = [];
   504→    try {
   505→      subTasks = await lookupCompletedSubTasksForOrchestrator(supabaseAdmin, task_id);
   506→    } catch (subTaskLookupError) {
   507→      const stage = subTaskLookupError instanceof SubTaskLookupError
   508→        ? subTaskLookupError.stage
   509→        : 'unknown';
   510→      const errorCode = stage === 'canonical'
   511→        ? 'subtask_query_failed_canonical'
   512→        : stage === 'legacy'
   513→          ? 'subtask_query_failed_legacy'
   514→          : 'subtask_query_failed';
   515→      logger.error("Failed to query sub-task references", {
   516→        error: subTaskLookupError instanceof Error
   517→          ? subTaskLookupError.message
   518→          : String(subTaskLookupError),
   519→        stage,
   520→      });
   521→      await logger.flush();
   522→      return errorResponse(errorCode, 'Failed to query sub-task references', 500, true);
   523→    }
   524→
   525→    let durationSeconds;
   526→    if (subTasks && subTasks.length > 0) {
   527→      // This is an orchestrator task with sub-tasks - sum their durations
   528→      logger.info("Orchestrator task detected", { sub_task_count: subTasks.length });
   529→
   530→      let totalSubTaskDuration = 0;
   531→      for (const subTask of subTasks) {
   532→        if (subTask.generation_started_at && subTask.generation_processed_at) {
   533→          const subStartTime = new Date(subTask.generation_started_at);
   534→          const subEndTime = new Date(subTask.generation_processed_at);
   535→          const subDuration = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((subEndTime.getTime() - subStartTime.getTime()) / 1000));
   536→          totalSubTaskDuration += subDuration;
   537→        }
   538→      }
   539→
   540→      durationSeconds = totalSubTaskDuration;
   541→      logger.debug("Orchestrator duration calculated", { 
   542→        sub_task_count: subTasks.length, 
   543→        total_duration_seconds: durationSeconds 
   544→      });
   545→    } else {
   546→      // Regular task - use its own duration
   547→      const startTime = new Date(typedTask.generation_started_at);
   548→      const endTime = new Date(typedTask.generation_processed_at);
   549→      durationSeconds = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((endTime.getTime() - startTime.getTime()) / 1000));
   550→    }
   551→
   552→    // Idempotency: avoid double-billing if this function is called multiple times for the same task.
   553→    // The credits ledger is intended to be immutable, so we should skip if a spend entry already exists.
   554→    const { data: existingSpendEntries, error: existingSpendError } = await supabaseAdmin
   555→      .from('credits_ledger')
   556→      .select('id, amount, created_at')
   557→      .eq('task_id', typedTask.id)
   558→      .eq('type', 'spend')
   559→      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
   560→      .limit(1);
   561→
   562→    if (existingSpendError) {
   563→      logger.error("Failed to check existing credit ledger entries", { error: existingSpendError.message });
   564→      await logger.flush();
   565→      return errorResponse('cost_record_check_failed', 'Failed to check existing cost records', 500, true);
   566→    }
   567→
   568→    if (existingSpendEntries && existingSpendEntries.length > 0) {
   569→      const existing = existingSpendEntries[0];
   570→      logger.info("Cost already recorded - skipping", {
   571→        ledger_id: existing.id,
   572→        existing_amount: existing.amount
   573→      });
   574→      await logger.flush();
   575→      return jsonResponse({
   576→        success: true,
   577→        skipped: true,
   578→        reason: 'Cost already recorded for this task',
   579→        task_id: typedTask.id,
   580→        ledger_id: existing.id,
   581→        existing_amount: existing.amount
   582→      });
   583→    }
   584→
   585→    // Get task type configuration (already joined via FK in the task query)
   586→    const taskType = typedTask.task_type_config;
   587→
   588→    if (!taskType || !taskType.is_active) {
   589→      logger.error("No task type config found, using defaults", { task_type: typedTask.task_type });
   590→
   591→      // Use default cost if no config found — must match DB-level get_task_cost() default of 0.0278
   592→      const defaultCostPerSecond = 0.0278;
   593→      const cost = defaultCostPerSecond * durationSeconds;
   594→
   595→      const { error: ledgerError } = await supabaseAdmin.from('credits_ledger').insert({
   596→        user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id,
   597→        task_id: typedTask.id,
   598→        amount: -cost,
   599→        type: 'spend',
   600→        metadata: {
   601→          task_type: typedTask.task_type,
   602→          duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   603→          base_cost_per_second: defaultCostPerSecond,
   604→          billing_type: 'per_second',
   605→          calculated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   606→          cost_fallback_used: true,
   607→          note: 'Default cost used - no task type configuration found'
   608→        }
   609→      });
   610→
   611→      if (ledgerError) {
   612→        logger.error("Failed to insert into credit ledger (default)", { error: ledgerError.message });
   613→        await logger.flush();
   614→        return errorResponse('ledger_write_failed', 'Failed to record cost in ledger', 500, true);
   615→      }
   616→
   617→      logger.info("Cost calculated (default rates)", { 
   618→        cost,
   619→        duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   620→        billing_type: 'per_second'
   621→      });
   622→      await logger.flush();
   623→
   624→      return jsonResponse({
   625→        success: true,
   626→        cost: cost,
   627→        duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   628→        base_cost_per_second: defaultCostPerSecond,
   629→        billing_type: 'per_second',
   630→        note: 'Default cost used - no task type configuration found'
   631→      });
   632→    }
   633→
   634→    // Calculate cost based on task type configuration
   635→    const costResult = calculateTaskCost(
   636→      typedTask.task_type,
   637→      taskType.billing_type,
   638→      taskType.base_cost_per_second,
   639→      taskType.unit_cost,
   640→      durationSeconds,
   641→      taskType.cost_factors,
   642→      typedTask.params
   643→    );
   644→    const cost = costResult.cost;
   645→    const costBreakdown = costResult.breakdown;
   646→
   647→    // Validate cost calculation
   648→    if (isNaN(cost) || cost < 0) {
   649→      logger.error("Invalid cost calculated", {
   650→        cost,
   651→        billing_type: taskType.billing_type,
   652→        base_cost_per_second: taskType.base_cost_per_second,
   653→        unit_cost: taskType.unit_cost,
   654→        duration: durationSeconds,
   655→        breakdown: costBreakdown
   656→      });
   657→      await logger.flush();
   658→      return errorResponse('invalid_cost_calculation', 'Invalid cost calculation', 500);
   659→    }
   660→
   661→    // Ensure user exists before inserting credit ledger entry
   662→    const { data: user, error: userError } = await supabaseAdmin
   663→      .from('users')
   664→      .select('id')
   665→      .eq('id', typedTask.projects.user_id)
   666→      .single();
   667→
   668→    if (userError || !user) {
   669→      logger.error("User not found for credit ledger", { 
   670→        user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id, 
   671→        error: userError?.message 
   672→      });
   673→      await logger.flush();
   674→      return errorResponse('billing_user_not_found', 'User not found for credit calculation', 400);
   675→    }
   676→
   677→    // Insert cost into credit ledger
   678→    const { error: ledgerError } = await supabaseAdmin.from('credits_ledger').insert({
   679→      user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id,
   680→      task_id: typedTask.id,
   681→      amount: -cost,
   682→      type: 'spend',
   683→      metadata: {
   684→        task_type: typedTask.task_type,
   685→        billing_type: taskType.billing_type,
   686→        duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   687→        base_cost_per_second: taskType.base_cost_per_second,
   688→        unit_cost: taskType.unit_cost,
   689→        cost_factors: taskType.cost_factors,
   690→        task_params: typedTask.params,
   691→        calculated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   692→        task_type_id: taskType.id,
   693→        // Include breakdown for compound pricing (e.g., video_enhance)
   694→        ...(costBreakdown ? { cost_breakdown: costBreakdown } : {})
   695→      }
   696→    });
   697→
   698→    if (ledgerError) {
   699→      logger.error("Failed to insert into credit ledger", { 
   700→        error: ledgerError.message,
   701→        user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id,
   702→        cost
   703→      });
   704→      await logger.flush();
   705→      return errorResponse('ledger_write_failed', 'Failed to record cost in ledger', 500, true);
   706→    }
   707→
   708→    logger.info("Cost calculated and recorded", {
   709→      cost,
   710→      billing_type: taskType.billing_type,
   711→      duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   712→      user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id,
   713→      ...(costBreakdown ? { breakdown: costBreakdown } : {})
   714→    });
   715→    await logger.flush();
   716→
   717→    return jsonResponse({
   718→      success: true,
   719→      cost: cost,
   720→      billing_type: taskType.billing_type,
   721→      duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   722→      base_cost_per_second: taskType.base_cost_per_second,
   723→      unit_cost: taskType.unit_cost,
   724→      cost_factors: taskType.cost_factors,
   725→      task_type: typedTask.task_type,
   726→      task_id: typedTask.id,
   727→      // Include breakdown for compound pricing (e.g., video_enhance)
   728→      ...(costBreakdown ? { cost_breakdown: costBreakdown } : {})
   729→    });
   730→
   731→  } catch (error: unknown) {
   732→    const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
   733→    const errorStack = error instanceof Error ? error.stack?.substring(0, 500) : undefined;
   734→    logger.critical("Unexpected error", { error: errorMessage, stack: errorStack });
   735→    await logger.flush();
   736→    return errorResponse('internal_server_error', 'Internal server error', 500, false);
   737→  }
   738→});
   739→

1→-- ============================================================================
     2→-- SECURITY AUDIT FIXES - January 2026
     3→-- ============================================================================
     4→-- This migration addresses findings from the database security audit:
     5→-- 1. task_types table - Add RLS to ensure read-only access (no modifications)
     6→-- 2. shot_generations - Add optimized indexes for RLS policy joins
     7→--
     8→-- Note: shared_generations INSERT policy was already fixed in
     9→-- 20251016000001_add_missing_rls_policies.sql
    10→-- ============================================================================
    11→
    12→BEGIN;
    13→
    14→-- ============================================================================
    15→-- 1. TASK_TYPES TABLE - Enable RLS with read-only access
    16→-- ============================================================================
    17→-- The task_types table contains pricing configuration. Users need to READ
    18→-- this data (to see pricing) but should NEVER be able to modify it.
    19→-- Only service_role should be able to modify pricing.
    20→
    21→ALTER TABLE task_types ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
    22→
    23→-- Drop any existing policies
    24→DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "task_types_select_authenticated" ON task_types;
    25→DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "task_types_service_role_all" ON task_types;
    26→
    27→-- Policy: Authenticated users can only SELECT (read pricing data)
    28→CREATE POLICY "task_types_select_authenticated" ON task_types
    29→  FOR SELECT
    30→  TO authenticated
    31→  USING (true);
    32→
    33→-- Policy: Service role has full access (for admin operations)
    34→CREATE POLICY "task_types_service_role_all" ON task_types
    35→  FOR ALL
    36→  TO service_role
    37→  USING (true)
    38→  WITH CHECK (true);
    39→
    40→-- Note: No INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policies for authenticated users
    41→-- This means they cannot modify the table at all
    42→
    43→COMMENT ON POLICY "task_types_select_authenticated" ON task_types IS
    44→  'Security: Users can read pricing data but cannot modify it';
    45→
    46→-- ============================================================================
    47→-- 2. SHOT_GENERATIONS RLS PERFORMANCE - Optimized index for ownership joins
    48→-- ============================================================================
    49→-- The RLS policy on shot_generations does:
    50→--   EXISTS (
    51→--     SELECT 1 FROM shots s
    52→--     JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id
    53→--     WHERE s.id = shot_generations.shot_id
    54→--     AND p.user_id = auth.uid()
    55→--   )
    56→-- This requires efficient lookup from shot_id -> project_id -> user_id
    57→--
    58→-- We already have idx_shot_generations_shot_id_position, but let's add
    59→-- a covering index that includes just what the RLS check needs.
    60→
    61→-- Check if the index already exists before creating
    62→DO $$
    63→BEGIN
    64→  IF NOT EXISTS (
    65→    SELECT 1 FROM pg_indexes
    66→    WHERE indexname = 'idx_shot_generations_rls_check'
    67→  ) THEN
    68→    CREATE INDEX idx_shot_generations_rls_check
    69→      ON shot_generations(shot_id);
    70→  END IF;
    71→END $$;
    72→
    73→-- Also ensure shots table has efficient project_id lookup for the join
    74→DO $$
    75→BEGIN
    76→  IF NOT EXISTS (
    77→    SELECT 1 FROM pg_indexes
    78→    WHERE indexname = 'idx_shots_project_id_for_rls'
    79→  ) THEN
    80→    CREATE INDEX idx_shots_project_id_for_rls
    81→      ON shots(project_id, id);
    82→  END IF;
    83→END $$;
    84→
    85→-- And projects needs efficient user_id lookup
    86→DO $$
    87→BEGIN
    88→  IF NOT EXISTS (
    89→    SELECT 1 FROM pg_indexes
    90→    WHERE indexname = 'idx_projects_user_id_for_rls'
    91→  ) THEN
    92→    CREATE INDEX idx_projects_user_id_for_rls
    93→      ON projects(user_id, id);
    94→  END IF;
    95→END $$;
    96→
    97→COMMENT ON INDEX idx_shot_generations_rls_check IS
    98→  'Optimizes RLS policy ownership check joins';
    99→COMMENT ON INDEX idx_shots_project_id_for_rls IS
   100→  'Optimizes RLS policy ownership check: shots -> projects join';
```

1→import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     2→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     3→import { taskQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/tasks';
     4→
     5→export interface TaskTypeInfo {
     6→  id: string;
     7→  name: string;
     8→  content_type: string | null;
     9→  tool_type: string | null;
    10→  display_name: string;
    11→  category: string;
    12→  is_visible: boolean;
    13→  supports_progress: boolean;
    14→}
    15→
    16→/**
    17→ * Hook to fetch task type information including content_type
    18→ * @param taskType - The task type name to look up
    19→ * @returns Query result with task type information
    20→ */
    21→export const useTaskType = (taskType: string) => {
    22→  return useQuery({
    23→    [REDACTED](taskType),
    24→    queryFn: async (): Promise<TaskTypeInfo | null> => {
    25→      const { data, error } = await supabase().from('task_types')
    26→        .select('id, name, content_type, tool_type, display_name, category, is_visible, supports_progress')
    27→        .eq('name', taskType)
    28→        .maybeSingle();
    29→
    30→      if (error) {
    31→        console.warn(`Failed to fetch task type info for ${taskType}:`, error);
    32→        return null;
    33→      }
    34→
    35→      if (!data) {
    36→        return null;
    37→      }
    38→
    39→      return {
    40→        ...data,
    41→        is_visible: data.is_visible ?? false,
    42→        supports_progress: data.supports_progress ?? false,
    43→      };
    44→    },
    45→    enabled: !!taskType,
    46→    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes - task types don't change often
    47→    gcTime: 10 * 60 * 1000, // 10 minutes
    48→  });
    49→};
    50→
    51→// =============================================================================
    52→// GLOBAL TASK TYPE CONFIG CACHE
    53→// =============================================================================
    54→
    55→// Global cache for synchronous access (populated by useAllTaskTypesConfig hook)
    56→let _taskTypeConfigCache: Record<string, TaskTypeInfo> = {};
    57→let _cacheInitialized = false;
    58→
    59→/**
    60→ * Get the global task type config cache
    61→ * Used by taskConfig.ts for synchronous access
    62→ */
    63→export function getTaskTypeConfigCache(): Record<string, TaskTypeInfo> {
    64→  return _taskTypeConfigCache;
    65→}
    66→
    67→/**
    68→ * Check if the cache has been initialized
    69→ */
    70→export function isTaskTypeConfigCacheInitialized(): boolean {
    71→  return _cacheInitialized;
    72→}
    73→
    74→/**
    75→ * Fetch all task types config directly (for initialization)
    76→ * This is called once on app load to populate the cache
    77→ */
    78→async function fetchAllTaskTypesConfig(): Promise<Record<string, TaskTypeInfo>> {
    79→  const { data, error } = await supabase().from('task_types')
    80→    .select('id, name, content_type, tool_type, display_name, category, is_visible, supports_progress')
    81→    .eq('is_active', true);
    82→
    83→  if (error) {
    84→    return {};
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  const configMap = (data || []).reduce((acc, taskType) => {
    88→    acc[taskType.name] = {
    89→      ...taskType,
    90→      is_visible: taskType.is_visible ?? false,
    91→      supports_progress: taskType.supports_progress ?? false,
    92→    };
    93→    return acc;
    94→  }, {} as Record<string, TaskTypeInfo>);
    95→
    96→  // Update the global cache
    97→  _taskTypeConfigCache = configMap;
    98→  _cacheInitialized = true;
    99→  
   100→  return configMap;
   101→}
   102→
   103→/**
   104→ * Hook to fetch and cache ALL task type configs
   105→ * Should be called once near the app root to initialize the cache
   106→ * @returns Query result with all task type configurations
   107→ */
   108→export const useAllTaskTypesConfig = () => {
   109→  return useQuery({
   110→    [REDACTED],
   111→    queryFn: fetchAllTaskTypesConfig,
   112→    staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000, // 10 minutes - task types config rarely changes
   113→    gcTime: 30 * 60 * 1000, // 30 minutes
   114→    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
   115→    refetchOnMount: false,
   116→  });
   117→};
   118→

    25→
    26→/**
    27→ * Best-effort mark a task as Failed so the UI can update immediately.
    28→ * Swallows errors — callers still return their own HTTP error response.
    29→ */
    30→async function markTaskFailed(
    31→  supabase: SupabaseClient,
    32→  taskId: string,
    33→  errorMessage: string,
    34→): Promise<void> {
    35→  try {
    36→    await supabase.from("tasks").update({
    37→      status: "Failed",
    38→      error_message: errorMessage,
    39→      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    40→    }).eq("id", taskId).in("status", ["Queued", "In Progress"]);
    41→  } catch {
    42→    // Best-effort — don't mask the original error
    43→  }
    44→}
    45→
    46→interface TaskContext {
    47→  id: string;
    48→  task_type: string;
    49→  project_id: string;
    50→  params: Record<string, unknown>;
    51→  tool_type: string;
    52→  category: string;
    53→  content_type: 'image' | 'video';
    54→  variant_type: string | null;
    55→}
    56→
    57→/**
    58→ * Fetch task context with all required fields for the completion flow.
    59→ * Uses a single query with FK join (tasks.task_type -> task_types.name).
    60→ * Returns null if task not found or on error.
    61→ */
    62→async function fetchTaskContext(
    63→  supabase: SupabaseClient,
    64→  taskId: string,
    65→  logger?: {
    66→    error: (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    67→  },
    68→): Promise<TaskContext | null> {
    69→  const { data: task, error } = await supabase
    70→    .from("tasks")
    71→    .select(`id, task_type, project_id, params, task_types!tasks_task_type_fkey(tool_type, category, content_type, variant_type)`)
    72→    .eq("id", taskId)
    73→    .single();
    74→
    75→  if (error || !task) {
    76→    logger?.error('Failed to fetch task context', {
    77→      task_id: taskId,
    78→      fetch_error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    79→    });
    80→    return null;
    81→  }
    82→
    83→  const taskTypeInfo = task.task_types || {};
    84→
    85→  return {
    86→    id: task.id,
    87→    task_type: task.task_type,
    88→    project_id: task.project_id,
    89→    params: task.params || {},
    90→    tool_type: taskTypeInfo.tool_type || 'unknown',
    91→    category: taskTypeInfo.category || 'unknown',
    92→    content_type: taskTypeInfo.content_type || 'image',
    93→    variant_type: taskTypeInfo.variant_type || null,
    94→  };
    95→}
    96→
    97→/**
    98→ * Edge function: complete-task
    99→ * 
   100→ * Completes a task by uploading file data and updating task status.
   101→ * - Service-role key: can complete any task
   102→ * - User token: can only complete tasks they own
   103→ * 
   104→ * POST /functions/v1/complete-task
   105→ * Headers: Authorization: Bearer <JWT or PAT>
   106→ * 
   107→ * SUPPORTS THREE UPLOAD MODES:
   108→ * 
   109→ * MODE 1 (LEGACY - JSON with base64): 
   110→ *   Body: { task_id, file_data: "base64...", filename: "image.png", ... }
   111→ * 
   112→ * MODE 3 (PRE-SIGNED URL - Zero Memory):
   113→ *   Body: { task_id, storage_path: "user_id/tasks/{task_id}/filename", ... }
   114→ * 
   115→ * MODE 4 (REFERENCE EXISTING PATH):
   116→ *   Body: { task_id, storage_path: "user_id/filename", ... }
   117→ */
   118→export async function completeTaskHandler(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
   119→  const bootstrap = await bootstrapEdgeHandler(req, {
   120→    functionName: "complete-task",
   121→    logPrefix: "[COMPLETE-TASK]",
   122→    parseBody: "none",
   123→    auth: {
   124→      required: true,
   125→      options: { allowJwtUserAuth: true },
   126→    },
   127→    runtimeOptions: {
   128→      clientOptions: {
   129→        auth: {
   130→          autoRefreshToken: false,
   131→          persistSession: false,
   132→        },
   133→      },
   134→    },
   135→  });
   136→  if (!bootstrap.ok) {
   137→    return bootstrap.response;
   138→  }
   139→
   140→  const { supabaseAdmin, logger, auth } = bootstrap.value;
   141→
   142→  // 1) Parse and validate request
   143→  const parseResult = await parseCompleteTaskRequest(req);
   144→  if (!parseResult.success) {
   145→    return parseResult.response;
   146→  }
   147→  const parsedRequest = parseResult.data;
   148→  const taskIdString = parsedRequest.taskId;
   149→
   150→  logger.setDefaultTaskId?.(taskIdString);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.func_daily_task_stats()
RETURNS TABLE (
  date text,
  images_generated bigint,
  images_edited bigint,
  videos_generated bigint
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
  SELECT
    to_char(t.created_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC', 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS date,
    COUNT(*) FILTER (
      WHERE tt.tool_type = 'image-generation'
    ) AS images_generated,
    COUNT(*) FILTER (
      WHERE tt.content_type = 'image' AND tt.tool_type != 'image-generation'
    ) AS images_edited,
    COUNT(*) FILTER (
      WHERE tt.content_type = 'video'
        AND (tt.category = 'orchestration'
             OR tt.name IN ('animate_character', 'individual_travel_segment', 'video_enhance'))
    ) AS videos_generated
  FROM tasks t
  JOIN task_types tt ON tt.name = t.task_type
  WHERE t.status = 'Complete'
    AND t.created_at >= '2026-02-09T00:00:00Z'::timestamptz
  GROUP BY 1
  ORDER BY 1;
$$;

-- Schedule cron job (requires pg_cron and pg_net extensions)
SELECT cron.schedule(
  'discord_daily_stats',
  '0 9 * * *',
  $$
  SELECT net.http_post(
    url := current_setting('app.settings.supabase_url') || '/functions/v1/discord-daily-stats',
    headers := jsonb_build_object(
      'Content-Type', 'application/json',
```

FROM users u
    LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN tasks in_progress_tasks ON in_progress_tasks.project_id = p.id 
    WHERE u.credits > 0
  FROM tasks t
  JOIN projects p ON t.project_id = p.id
  JOIN eligible_users eu ON eu.user_id = p.user_id
  LEFT JOIN tasks dep ON t.dependant_on = dep.id
  WHERE t.status = ANY(v_status_filter)

```
SELECT 
      COALESCE(t.task_type, 'unknown') as task_type_name,
      COUNT(*)::INTEGER as type_count,
      COALESCE(tt.run_type, 'unknown') as task_run_type
    FROM tasks t
    LEFT JOIN task_types tt ON tt.name = t.task_type AND tt.is_active = true
    WHERE (p_include_active AND t.status IN ('Queued'::task_status, 'In Progress'::task_status))
       OR (NOT p_include_active AND t.status = 'Queued'::task_status)
    GROUP BY t.task_type, tt.run_type
  ),
  filtered_type_counts AS (
    SELECT * FROM task_type_counts
    WHERE p_run_type IS NULL OR task_run_type = p_run_type
  ),
  status_counts AS (
    -- Separate query for status counts
    SELECT 
      COUNT(*)::INTEGER as total,
      COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE t.status = 'Queued'::task_status)::INTEGER as queued,
      COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE t.status = 'In Progress'::task_status)::INTEGER as in_progress
    FROM tasks t
    LEFT JOIN task_types tt ON tt.name = t.task_type AND tt.is_active = true
    WHERE (p_include_active AND t.status IN ('Queued'::task_status, 'In Progress'::task_status))
       OR (NOT p_include_active AND t.status = 'Queued'::task_status)
      AND (p_run_type IS NULL OR tt.run_type = p_run_type)
  ),
  breakdown_agg AS (
    -- Now aggregate the pre-computed counts (single aggregate, not nested)
    SELECT COALESCE(
      jsonb_object_agg(task_type_name, type_count),
      '{}'::jsonb
    ) as breakdown
    FROM filtered_type_counts
  )
  SELECT 
    sc.total,
    sc.queued,
```

## FINDINGS REPORT: task_types FK Join Optimization

### Summary
I searched the entire codebase for unnecessary separate task_types queries that could use the FK join instead. **Only ONE location requires optimization.**

### LOCATION NEEDING OPTIMIZATION

#### 1. **`supabase/functions/task-counts/index.ts` (Lines 177-188)**
**Status:** NEEDS CONVERSION

```typescript
// Current pattern: Separate query for ALL task_types to build a lookup map
let taskTypeRunTypeMap: Map<string, string> | null = null;
if (runType) {
  const { data: taskTypes } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from('task_types')
    .select('name, run_type')
    .eq('is_active', true);

  if (taskTypes) {
    taskTypeRunTypeMap = new Map(taskTypes.map(tt => [tt.name, tt.run_type]));
  }
}
```

**Issue:** This fetches ALL task_types when `runType` filter is specified. Then uses the map to filter queued/active tasks at lines 277-279 and 296-299.

**Why it's different from task-counts/index.ts:** This is intentional per the user's skip instructions - `task-counts` fetches ALL task_types into a Map for run_type filtering across multiple queries, which is appropriate for that use case.

#### 3. ✅ `supabase/migrations/20260205210535_add_discord_stats_cron.sql`
RPC function `func_daily_task_stats()` uses proper JOIN:
```sql
FROM tasks t
JOIN task_types tt ON tt.name = t.task_type
```

#### 4. ✅ `supabase/migrations/20260112000000_fix_analyze_task_availability_nested_aggregate.sql`
RPC function uses LEFT JOIN to fetch task_types info:
```sql
LEFT JOIN task_types tt ON tt.name = t.task_type AND tt.is_active = true
```

Frontend hook - correctly queries task_types separately because it's a single-task lookup (not a per-task follow-up).
